Abstract: The fusion of hyperspectral (HS) and Light Detection and Ranging (LiDAR) data plays a crucial role in enhancing land-cover classification by jointly exploiting spectral, spatial, and structural cues. However, existing multimodal fusion methods still struggle to model long-range dependencies and complex anisotropic interactions while maintaining computational efficiency. This paper introduces M2Heat, a physics-inspired framework that investigates multimodal fusion through the lens of heat conduction. At its core, a physics-driven visual heat conduction module (vHeat) and enhanced Frequency Value Embeddings (FVEs) simulate anisotropic information flow, enabling the capture of global dependencies with sub-quadratic complexity and physical interpretability. This mechanism, combined with a hybrid spatial-frequency fusion strategy named Cross-Frequency Fusion (CFF) module, produces highly discriminative and robust feature representations. M2Heat achieves competitive overall performance on three benchmarks, i.e., Trento, Houston2013, and Augsburg, while providing an interpretable heat-conduction-guided perspective for multimodal feature fusion. These results indicate the potential of heat-conduction-guided neural operators for efficient and interpretable RS multimodal fusion. The source code is publicly available at https: /github.com/Weikan0425/M2Heat_HSI_LiDAR.

</details>


### [19] [Meta-Learning for Classifier Selection in Image Datasets: A Feature-Driven Framework for Accuracy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11041)
*Zahra Nabizadeh_Shahre_Babak,Farzaneh Koohestani,Nader Karimi,Shahram Shirani,Shadrokh Samavi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个元学习框架，通过提取数据集复杂度元特征预测图像分类器性能，在56个图像数据集上平均排序预测准确率超过86%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无免费午餐定理表明不存在普遍最优的模型，而图像数据集的内在复杂性和多样性使选择最合适的分类器成为一项关键且困难的任务。穷举训练所有分类器代价高昂，因此需要一种无需完整训练即可预测分类器性能的方法。

Method: 提出元学习框架，构建捕捉数据集复杂度的综合元特征集合；采用自编码器、预训练网络和降维技术提取与选择特征；训练回归模型高效估计分类器准确率；并使用聚类技术将性能模式相似的分类器分组，以简化推荐过程。

Result: 在涵盖自然、动物、数字、摩托车、医学图像和人体等多种概念的56个图像数据集上评估，平均排序预测准确率超过86%。

Conclusion: 该可扩展且可解释的框架为改善分类性能并降低计算成本提供了实用解决方案，能有效指导模型选择。

Abstract: No Free Lunch theorem implies that any performance gains achieved by a classifier on a particular image distribution are necessarily offset by a loss of performance over the set of all possible problems; thus, no single model is universally optimal. Selecting the most suitable classifier for image datasets is a critical yet challenging task due to the intrinsic complexity and diversity of images. This paper proposes a meta-learning framework that leverages a comprehensive set of meta-features capturing dataset complexity to predict classifier performance without exhaustive training. By extracting and selecting features using methods such as autoencoders, pre-trained networks, and dimensionality reduction techniques, we train regression models to efficiently estimate classifier accuracies. Additionally, clustering techniques are employed to group classifiers with similar performance patterns, simplifying the recommendation process. The datasets used span a wide range of concepts, including nature, animals, numbers, motorcycles, medical images, and human bodies, to ensure broad generalization. Evaluated on 56 diverse image datasets, our approach achieves an average ranking prediction accuracy exceeding 86%, demonstrating its effectiveness in guiding model selection. This scalable and interpretable framework provides a practical solution to improve classification performance while reducing computational costs.

</details>


### [20] [TailProp: content-adaptive light- and heavy-tailed propagation for vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11081)
*Jiahao Kong,Zihan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出TailProp，一种基于尾部传播算子（TPO）的层次化视觉骨干网络，通过高斯与柯西稳定过程传播子的互补双基、内容条件化的通道系数进行跨机制自适应传播，并在DCT域高效融合，实现O(N^1.5)空间混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学启发的视觉模型虽能用显式传播动力学替代传统token mixing，但通常只在单一动力学族内构建和调整视觉传播，而视觉表示在不同样本、通道和网络阶段需要差异较大的空间交互，因此需要探索跨机制的自适应传播。

Method: 提出TailProp层次化视觉骨干，核心为尾部传播算子TPO：以高斯（快速衰减）和柯西（重尾）稳定过程传播子作为互补基，预测内容条件化的通道级系数自适应组合二者；由于该系数在空间上共享，可借助单对DCT/IDCT在DCT域直接融合两种响应，对N=HW的方形特征图实现O(N^1.5)的空间混合。

Result: 在图像分类、目标检测、语义分割、鲁棒性及跨骨干恢复等任务上，TailProp一致优于匹配的传播基线；TailProp-B在ImageNet-1K达84.4% Top-1，3x Mask R-CNN下为50.3/44.8 box/mask AP，ADE20K达50.8% mIoU。受控消融表明增益并非来自单基传播、同族附加分支或族内自适应阶数。

Conclusion: 互补双基传播是一种有效的视觉表示学习设计原则，跨机制自适应传播可带来一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Science-inspired vision models show that explicit propagation dynamics can provide structured and interpretable alternatives to conventional token mixing. Existing formulations, however, typically construct and adapt visual propagation within a particular dynamical family, while visual representations can require substantially different spatial interactions across samples, channels, and network stages. We explore cross-regime adaptive propagation and introduce TailProp, a hierarchical vision backbone built upon the Tail Propagation Operator (TPO). TPO uses Gaussian and Cauchy stable-process propagators as complementary bases with rapidly decaying and heavy-tailed spatial influence, and predicts a content-conditioned channel-wise coefficient to adaptively combine them. Because this coefficient is spatially shared, the two responses are fused directly in the DCT domain with a single DCT/IDCT pair, yielding $O(N^{1.5})$ spatial mixing for square feature maps with $N=HW$ and fixed channel width. Across image classification, object detection, semantic segmentation, robustness, and cross-backbone restoration, TailProp consistently outperforms matched propagation baselines; TailProp-B reaches 84.4% Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1K, 50.3/44.8 box/mask AP under the 3x Mask R-CNN schedule, and 50.8% mIoU on ADE20K. Controlled ablations further show that these gains are not explained by single-basis propagation, an additional same-family branch, or within-family adaptive order alone, supporting complementary two-basis propagation as an effective design principle for visual representation learning.

</details>


### [21] [Beyond Benchmarks: Using VLMs to Reveal Systematic Classification Failures Under Real World Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11126)
*Dieuwertje Alblas,Alma M. Liezenga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Fedor Taggenbrock,Dalia Aljawaheri,Klamer Schutte*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文初步评估了基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的误差切片检测方法在国防应用分类模型验证与确认中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分类模型的验证与确认（V&amp;V）目前依赖耗时的人工检查错误样本来发现系统性错误模式。国防领域还面临两个额外挑战：VLM训练数据缺乏国防领域样本，且国防场景的环境与上下文多样性较低，因此需要评估VLM方法在该领域的适用性。

Method: 提出一种基于VLM的误差切片检测（ESD）方法，利用VLM将图像嵌入语义向量空间，独立地对分类模型的系统性错误进行分组与标注；在非军事数据集（含人为添加扰动）和军事数据集上分别进行实验。

Result: 在非军事数据集上，方法能识别出具有实际运行意义的、人为添加的扰动；在军事场景中，方法能依据环境背景对图像进行聚类和描述，但聚类描述之间存在重叠；同时发现军事与非军事数据集的嵌入变化存在差异，值得进一步研究。

Conclusion: 现有结果尚不足以支持完全自动化的VLM误差切片检测式V&amp;V，但表明VLM未来可用于加速验证与确认流程。

Abstract: Verification and validation (V&V) of classification models is crucial to enable a wide range of sensor processing applications. Currently, the V&V process relies on time-consuming manual inspection of erroneous samples to find meaningful patterns. This work explores the use of Vision Language Models (VLMs) to speed up this laborious process. VLMs are trained to embed images into a semantically meaningful vector representation, from which human-interpretable systematic errors can be distilled. Deploying such VLM-based methods in a defence context introduces two major challenges: (1) the defence domain is underrepresented in the training data of VLMs, and (2) surroundings and context are less diverse than for other domains. This study provides an initial assessment of the suitability of VLM-based methods for V&V of defence applications. We propose a VLM-based error slice detection (ESD) method that independently groups and labels systematic errors made by a classification model. We demonstrate that this method is able to identify operationally-relevant artificially added perturbations in a non-military dataset. In a military context, our method clusters and describes images based on their surroundings, but also exhibits overlap between cluster descriptions. We further investigate the difference in embedding variation between our military and non-military dataset, which remains a topic of interest. Although the results do not yet warrant fully automated V&V through VLM-based ESD, they show that VLMs could be used to accelerate V&V processes in the future.

</details>


### [22] [ReconPlusGen: Injecting Reconstruction Prior into Multi-view 3D Generation through Noise Inversion and Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11129)
*Jiarui Liu,Heng Li,Weiyu Li,Keng Deng,Junyuan Deng,Zheng Zhongxing,Junyu Huang,Jiahao Chang,Xiaoguang Han,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该内容为论文的定性结果展示与核心思想图示说明，而非完整摘要，展示了基准图像与真实图像的重建效果，以及"重建引导的噪声初始化与调制"方法流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 说明如何利用输入图像预测的几何信息引导扩散过程，以兼顾几何准确性与生成灵活性。

Method: 给定多张输入图像，预测规范空间中的点云；通过噪声反演将预测几何确定性地注入扩散过程，并对所得噪声进行调制，以保留补全未观测区域和优化可见几何所需的生成灵活性。

Result: 展示了在基准图像上的重建结果，以及在真实世界图像上的重建结果。

Conclusion: 重建引导的噪声初始化与调制能够将确定性几何先验与扩散模型的生成能力结合，实现更完整的重建效果。

Abstract: Qualitative results and an illustration of our core idea. Top left: reconstruction results on benchmark images. Top right: reconstruction results on real-world images. Bottom: illustration of reconstruction-guided noise initialization and modulation. Given multiple input images, we predict a point cloud in canonical space, deterministically inject the predicted geometry into the diffusion process through noise inversion, and modulate the resulting noise to preserve the generative flexibility required to complete unobserved regions and refine visible geometry.

</details>


### [23] [LAION-Mobile: Evaluating Deepfake Detectors On One Million Smartphone Photos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11134)
*Achim von Stryk,Janis Keuper*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出LAION-Mobile数据集（约100万张带EXIF的手机图像），用12个先进深度伪造检测器评估发现：在现代AI内容上无一超过AUC 0.624，且真实照片误报率是阈值校准的产物，没有检测器能同时兼顾两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在其基准上近乎完美，但近期ICML立场论文指出这些评估忽视了现代智能手机摄影的影响：设备端神经ISP管线使成像从简单镜头投影转向计算摄影，设备是"生成"而非"记录"照片，可能导致检测器将普通手机照片误判为伪造。由于缺乏大规模现代智能手机图像数据集，该假设此前仅在小规模概念验证中检验。

Method: 构建LAION-Mobile开放数据集，包含约100万张从re-LAION-5B中提炼的带EXIF元数据的智能手机图像。使用12个先进深度伪造检测器及其原始论文检查点，在9,115张图像的评估样本（DIRE用738张）上评估。

Result: 三项关键发现：(i) 在现代AI内容上无检测器AUC超过0.624，12个中有5个低于随机水平；(ii) 真实照片误报率是阈值校准的产物——在遗留GAN数据上拟合的阈值使若干检测器看似可用（FPR<11%），但同一标准在现代内容上重新拟合后，这些检测器会误报17-91%的真实照片；(iii) 因此没有检测器能既在现代AI内容上超越随机水平，又保持可部署的真实照片误报率。

Conclusion: 该语料库镜像了网络收集的设备构成，探测的是第一代神经ISP（2018-2020），当前旗舰机型基本缺席，现代ISP场景仍是待填补的空白。

Abstract: Most Deepfake detectors report near-perfect AUC scores on their reference benchmarks. However, a recent ICML position paper argues that these evaluations collectively neglect the impact of modern smartphone photography: the widely used on-device neural image-signal processing pipelines (like multi-sensor fusion or noise and motion-blur suppression) increasingly shift the imaging paradigm from simple lens projections towards computational photography. Hence, devices actually generate, rather than record photos. This increases the risk that deepfake detectors may flag ordinary phone photos as fake. Due to the lack of large-scale datasets containing images from modern smartphones, this hypothesis has so far only been tested in small proof-of-concept studies. The aim of this paper is to close this gap. We introduce LAION-Mobile, an open dataset containing about 1 million smartphone images with EXIF metadata distilled from re-LAION-5B. Evaluating twelve state-of-the-art deepfake detectors with their original paper checkpoints on a 9,115-image evaluation sample of this pool (DIRE on 738), we report three key findings: (i) On modern AI content no detector exceeds AUC 0.624, and five of twelve fall below chance. (ii) Real-photo false-alarm rates are an artefact of threshold calibration: thresholds fitted on legacy GAN data make several detectors look deployable (less than 11 percent FPR), yet the same detectors flag 17-91 percent of real photos once the identical criterion is refit on modern content. (iii) Consequently, no detector both beats chance on modern AI content and keeps a deployable real-photo false-alarm rate. Mirroring the device mix of web collections, the corpus probes the first neural-ISP generation (2018-2020); current flagships are essentially absent, leaving the modern-ISP regime as the open gap.

</details>


### [24] [UniH$^3$: Unifying Hierarchical Homogeneity and Heterogeneity for All-in-One Medical Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11156)
*Zhiwen Yang,Jiayin Li,Chengyu Liu,Hui Zhang,Bingzheng Wei,Yan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 UniH3 框架，通过统一层级同质性与异质性实现一体化医学图像复原，在多个基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有一体化医学图像复原方法多聚焦于任务间异质性（如数据分布与退化类型差异），却忽视了医学图像固有的同质性（如跨模态共享的解剖结构），而该同质性可用于降低训练难度并提升泛化能力。

Method: 提出 UniH3 框架：1) 层级同质性记忆（H2M）模块，在训练中逐步从高质量图像蒸馏任务内/任务间同质性先验，并针对输入自适应检索最相关先验以引导复原；2) 同质性引导注意力（HGA）机制，将检索到的先验高效注入复原流程；3) 层级异质性平衡器（H2B），缓解优化过程中的任务间与任务内冲突，实现均衡有效的多任务学习。

Result: 在 MedIR-2D-500K 与 MedIR-3D-3K 两个大规模基准上，UniH3 在一体化及单任务医学图像复原任务中均取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: UniH3 同时建模同质性与异质性，为通用医学图像复原模型建立了强基准并推动其发展，代码已开源。

Abstract: All-in-One medical image restoration (MedIR) aims to address diverse tasks across modalities and degradation types using a single universal model. Existing methods typically prioritize modeling inter-task heterogeneity (e.g., distinct data distributions and degradation types). However, they largely neglect the inherent homogeneity present in medical images, such as widely shared anatomical structures within and across modalities, which can be leveraged to ease model training and improve generalization. To this end, we propose UniH3, a novel framework that Unifies Hierarchical Homogeneity and Heterogeneity for all-in-one medical image restoration. Specifically, to comprehensively exploit homogeneity, we introduce a Hierarchical Homogeneity Memory (H2M) module that progressively distills intra- and inter-task homogeneity priors from high-quality images during training, and adaptively retrieves the most relevant priors tailored to the input for guided restoration. These retrieved priors are then injected into the restoration pipeline via an efficient Homogeneity-Guided Attention (HGA) mechanism. Furthermore, to comprehensively address heterogeneity, we design a Hierarchical Heterogeneity Balancer (H2B) that mitigates both inter- and intra-task conflicts during optimization, facilitating balanced and effective multi-task learning. Extensive experiments on two large-scale benchmarks, MedIR-2D-500K and MedIR-3D-3K, demonstrate that UniH3 achieves state-of-the-art performance on both all-in-one and single-task medical image restoration. We hope this work establishes a strong benchmark and advances the development of general-purpose medical image restoration models. Code is available at https://github.com/Yaziwel/UniH3.

</details>


### [25] [Beyond Visual Quality: Evaluating Physical Consistency under Ego-Motion with EgoGenEval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11172)
*Yilin Long,Chenming Zhu,Zitang Gou,Jingli Lin,Tai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出EgoGenEval基准，评估视觉生成器在自我运动下的物理一致性，包含1400个案例与2360个目标视角，分别度量相机运动定位（CMG）与场景状态保持（SSP）。测试16个无位姿生成器与2个位姿条件参考模型，发现无系统能同时兼顾两轴。基于同一几何流程构建EgoGen-Train进行SFT研究，发现成对监督无法可靠同时提升两项能力，指出成对教师强制目标本身是瓶颈，需转向轨迹中心范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成器虽能生成高保真图像，但在自我运动下常违反物理一致性，限制了其在空间推理与具身规划中的应用。而现有基准多聚焦孤立图像或单步质量，该挑战尚未被充分探索，因此需要一个几何接地的无位姿基准来评估该能力。

Method: 研究分两部分：(1) 构建EgoGenEval，含1400个案例、2360个目标视角，覆盖单步与多步自我运动，分别度量相机运动定位（CMG）与场景状态保持（SSP），两项指标均经盲评人类判断验证；评估16个无位姿生成器与2个位姿条件参考模型。(2) 用同一几何接地流程构建EgoGen-Train，开展受控SFT研究，考察基准衍生数据能否提升这些能力。

Result: 评估显示当前模型难以在执行相机运动的同时保持场景状态，无系统能在两轴上同时表现良好。SFT研究显示成对监督无法可靠地同时提升相机运动定位与场景状态保持：即使在全训练集与最长预算下，场景保持的提升也仅为相机运动提升的一小部分。

Conclusion: 成对教师强制目标本身是绑定约束，这促使研究转向轨迹中心范式，将自条件化rollout与显式位姿和可见性监督相结合。

Abstract: Recent visual generators produce high-fidelity images yet often violate physical consistency under ego-motion, limiting their use for spatial reasoning and embodied planning. Existing benchmarks largely focus on isolated images or single-step quality, leaving this challenge underexplored. We introduce EgoGenEval, a geometry-grounded, pose-free benchmark designed to evaluate the physical consistency of visual generators under ego-motion, and organize our study into two parts. (1) EgoGenEval contains 1,400 cases and 2,360 target views spanning single-step and multi-step ego-motion. It separately measures Camera Motion Grounding (CMG) and Scene State Preservation (SSP), with both metrics validated against blinded human judgments. Evaluating 16 pose-free generators together with two pose-conditioned references reveals that current models struggle to execute camera motion while maintaining scene state, and that no system performs well on both axes at once. (2) To examine whether benchmark-derived data can improve these capabilities, we build EgoGen-Train from the same geometry-grounded pipeline and run controlled SFT studies. These show that pairwise supervision does not reliably improve camera-motion grounding and scene-state preservation together: even at the full training pool and the longest budget, scene preservation gains a fraction of what camera motion does. This points to the pairwise teacher-forced objective itself as the binding constraint, motivating a trajectory-centric paradigm that couples self-conditioned rollouts with explicit pose and visibility supervision.

</details>


### [26] [A Multi-View and Confusion-Guided Ensemble Framework for Robust Synthetic Image Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11188)
*Zuomin Qu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多视角与混淆引导的集成框架，用于合成图像溯源（SIA）挑战，结合多种架构并通过专用二分类专家与置信度校准解决易混淆类别，在公开/私有榜单分别取得99.53%与99.20%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着文本到图像生成模型的快速发展，合成图像溯源变得日益重要。然而，由于现代扩散模型生成器之间相似度不断提高以及多样化后处理操作的存在，准确识别生成图像的来源模型仍具挑战。

Method: 提出多视角与混淆引导的集成框架，整合FFT-ConvNeXt、DINOv2、CLIP、Xception等互补架构，从频率、语义与取证视角捕捉溯源线索；训练时采用大量数据增强模拟压缩、缩放、灰度化、模糊等真实后处理；分析集成模型的混淆模式，针对Stable Diffusion 3与3.5的严重歧义引入专用二分类专家分类器并在低置信度条件下选择性激活；同时应用类别自适应置信度校准以提升如腾讯混元等困难类别的区分度。

Result: 该框架在公开榜单上取得99.53%的准确率，在私有榜单上取得99.20%的准确率。源代码与实现细节已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: 多视角集成结合混淆引导的专家分类器与置信度校准，能有效应对合成图像溯源中模型相似与后处理干扰带来的挑战，显著提升识别性能。

Abstract: Synthetic image attribution (SIA) has become increasingly important with the rapid advancement of text-to-image generation models. However, accurately identifying the source model of a generated image remains challenging due to the growing similarity among modern diffusion-based generators and the presence of diverse post-processing operations. In this report, we present a multi-view and confusion-guided ensemble framework for the Synthetic Image Attribution Challenge of the DLMMDD Workshop at ICANN 2026. Our approach integrates multiple complementary architectures, including FFT-ConvNeXt, DINOv2, CLIP, and Xception, to capture diverse attribution cues from frequency, semantic, and forensic perspectives. To improve robustness against unknown degradations and image manipulations, extensive data augmentation strategies are employed during training, simulating realistic post-processing operations such as compression, resizing, grayscale conversion, and blur. Furthermore, we analyze the confusion patterns of the ensemble model and observe severe ambiguity between Stable Diffusion 3 and Stable Diffusion 3.5. To address this issue, we introduce a dedicated binary expert classifier that is selectively activated under low-confidence conditions. We additionally apply class-adaptive confidence calibration to improve the discrimination of challenging classes such as Tencent Hunyuan. The proposed framework achieved 99.53% on the public leaderboard and 99.20% on the private leaderboard. The source code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/ZOMIN28/SIA.

</details>


### [27] [CEM-TUDASR: Computationally efficient multi-modality transformer based unsupervised domain adaptive super-resolution approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11201)
*Anjali Sarvaiya,Jay Kadel,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CEM-TUDASR，一种计算高效的无监督Transformer超分辨率框架，用于无线胶囊内镜图像增强，无需配对训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线胶囊内镜（WCE）因微型光学、传感器限制和无线传输约束，图像分辨率低，诊断重要结构可见性差，亟需图像增强。

Method: 采用域自适应退化网络，从高清常规内镜图像合成类WCE低分辨率图像以缩小域差距；SR生成器集成深度注意力块（DAB）与融合注意力块（FAB），捕捉长程上下文依赖与局部细节；使用Kvasir Capsule数据集训练，在KID和GIANA上评估跨数据集泛化。

Result: 在BRISQUE、PIQE、NIQE及领域专用EndoQM等无参考指标上持续优于现有无监督SR方法；定性结果显示黏膜纹理、血管模式和解剖细节恢复更佳；跨域视网膜图像实验验证了适应性；仅2.67M参数、169.94 GFLOPs。

Conclusion: CEM-TUDASR在保持计算效率的同时实现高质量重建，适用于资源受限的临床与嵌入式内镜应用。

Abstract: Wireless Capsule Endoscopy (WCE) enables non-invasive visualization of the gastrointestinal tract, but its miniaturized optics, sensor limitations, and wireless transmission constraints result in low-resolution images with reduced visibility of diagnostically important structures. This paper proposes CEM-TUDASR, a computationally efficient unsupervised Transformer-based super-resolution framework for WCE image enhancement without paired low-resolution (LR) and high-resolution (HR) training data. A domain-adaptive degradation network synthesizes realistic WCE-like LR images from HR conventional endoscopy images, reducing the domain gap and enabling effective unpaired learning. The SR generator integrates Deep Attention Blocks (DABs) and a Fusion Attention Block (FAB) to capture long-range contextual dependencies and fine local structures while preserving perceptual and structural fidelity. The model is trained on a curated dataset derived from Kvasir Capsule and evaluated on KID and GIANA for cross-dataset generalization. No-reference quality metrics, including BRISQUE, PIQE, NIQE, and the domain-specific EndoQM, show that CEM-TUDASR consistently outperforms existing unsupervised SR methods. Qualitative results further demonstrate improved restoration of mucosal textures, vascular patterns, and clinically relevant anatomical details. Cross-domain experiments on retinal images additionally demonstrate the adaptability of the framework. With only 2.67 million parameters and 169.94 GFLOPs, CEM-TUDASR achieves high-quality reconstruction while maintaining computational efficiency, making it suitable for resource-constrained clinical and embedded endoscopic applications.

</details>


### [28] [Tri-DehazeGS: Scene--Medium Decoupled Gaussian Splatting with Transmittance-Aware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11223)
*Kui Jiang,Yang Gu,Jiacheng Liu,Shiyu Liu,Youyu Chen,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Tri-DehazeGS，通过场景-介质解耦的3D高斯泼溅框架与透射率感知优化，提升雾霾多视角图像下的干净新视角重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有散射感知高斯泼溅方法在图像空间施加退化或将介质变量绑定到高斯原语上，导致干净场景辐射与大气效应纠缠；同时低透射率区域监督弱，远处或浓雾区域重建不足。

Method: 用高斯原语表示干净场景，用独立视图共享的三平面场建模参与介质，通过物理散射模型合成雾霾观测；并引入介质解耦透射率梯度补偿（MD-TGC），在冻结介质后补偿被雾霾抑制的梯度，不改变前向渲染。

Result: 在真实与合成雾霾基准上，Tri-DehazeGS 改善了干净新视角重建效果。

Conclusion: 干净重建需同时实现场景-介质解耦与透射率感知的优化再平衡，该方法验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Recovering clean 3D scenes from hazy multi-view images is challenging because haze attenuates scene radiance and introduces atmospheric scattering. Recent scattering-aware Gaussian Splatting methods introduce physical haze models into reconstruction, but they often apply degradation in image space or bind medium-related variables to Gaussian primitives, which can entangle clean scene radiance with atmospheric effects. Moreover, low-transmittance regions provide weakened supervision for Gaussian optimization, causing distant or dense-haze areas to be under-reconstructed. We argue that clean reconstruction under haze requires both scene--medium disentanglement and transmittance-aware optimization rebalancing. To this end, we propose Tri-DehazeGS, a scene--medium decoupled Gaussian Splatting framework. It represents the clean scene with Gaussian primitives, models the participating medium using an independent view-shared tri-plane field, and composes hazy observations through a physical scattering model. We further introduce Medium-Decoupled Transmittance Gradient Compensation (MD-TGC), which compensates haze-suppressed gradients after medium freezing without altering forward rendering. Experiments on real and synthetic haze benchmarks show that Tri-DehazeGS improves clean novel-view reconstruction. Code is available at https://github.com/aptx46/Tri-DehazeGS.

</details>


### [29] [When is Test-Time Adaptation Identifiable From Unlabeled Evidence?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11235)
*Kartik Jhawar,Lipo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究测试时自适应（TTA）中选择更新的可辨识性问题，指出即使选择器完美，若观测通道无法区分具有不同TTA排序的部署，可靠选择也不可能；并在高斯TTA模型与公开基准上验证了这一边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTA方法多依赖无标签测试数据预测哪种自适应更有效，但很少追问所给证据是否本身包含足够信息来决定最佳动作。作者希望区分两种常被混淆的失败模式：选择器弱，还是信息通道根本无法支持该决策。

Method: 作者在有限批次高斯TTA模型中精确刻画可辨识边界，分析“不更新”与“均值重中心化”在不同偏移下的优劣；并在CIFAR-100-C与DomainNet-126上对现代TTA方法进行基准研究，仅改变部署结构以检验神谕动作是否反转而全局顺序无关证据是否不变。

Result: 理论上，小偏移时“不更新”优于均值重中心化，超过唯一临界偏移后重中心化占优，且该边界以1/√n缩小。实验中，仅改变部署结构即可反转神谕动作，而全局顺序无关证据保持不变，显示相同失败模式在现代TTA方法中也存在。

Conclusion: TTA选择问题存在信息层面的根本边界：当观测通道无法解决相关歧义时，完美选择器也无法可靠工作；更丰富的证据只有在消除该歧义时才能恢复决策。该结果为区分“弱选择器”与“信息不足通道”提供了实用判据。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) offers many ways to update a deployed model without labels, but choosing the wrong update can make a strong source model worse. Recent methods therefore try to predict which adaptation will work from unlabeled test data. We ask a prior question: does the evidence given to the selector contain enough information to determine the best action at all? We show that this is not guaranteed, even with a perfect selector. If an observation channel makes two deployments look the same while their TTA rankings differ, reliable selection is impossible from that channel; richer evidence can restore the decision only when it resolves the relevant ambiguity. We make this boundary exact in a finite-batch Gaussian TTA model, where doing nothing beats mean recentering for small shifts, recentering wins beyond a unique critical shift, and the boundary shrinks as $1/\sqrt n$. Public benchmark studies on CIFAR-100-C and DomainNet-126 show the same failure mode with modern TTA methods: changing only deployment structure can reverse the oracle action while global order-blind evidence remains unchanged. The result is a practical way to separate two failure modes that are usually mixed together: a weak selector versus an information channel that cannot support the desired decision in the first place.

</details>


### [30] [HALDETECT at ImageEval 2026 Shared Tasks: Answer-First Contrastive Grounding with QLoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11236)
*Syed Mohaiminul Hoque,Md Sakhawat Hossain*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍 HALDETECT 系统，用于 ImageEval 2026 英文幻觉检测任务（Task 1b），通过对比式决策与结构化推理，微调 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 检测多模态模型的文化相关视觉幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型多模态模型常流畅地"幻觉"出视觉细节，限制了其在细粒度解读上的部署；该任务要求系统从图像与三条文化上合理的陈述中识别唯一有视觉依据的一条。

Method: 将任务构建为单一对比决策，先输出答案再给出解释，围绕颜色/纹理、形状/形式与语境组织推理；对 Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct 采用 4-bit QLoRA 微调并冻结视觉编码器。

Result: 最佳提交适配器在 1,000 条测试集上取得对比不稳定性（CI）0.035，在八支队伍中排名第三。开发实验显示答案顺序的影响可能大于模型规模，适配优于单纯提示。回顾性配对分析证实 QLoRA 优于最佳提示，但未能证实 devtest 选择与最佳测试适配器之间的微小差距；对四种训练规模重新设置随机种子表明，表面上的数据规模曲线在换种子后并不成立。35 个残余错误属于文化上合理的功能、材料与识别类区分，简单适配器投票无帮助。

Conclusion: 适配（QLoRA）优于纯提示，但部分经验性发现（如数据规模效应、适配器间微小差距）在重设随机种子后不稳健；残余错误集中于文化相关的细粒度语义区分。

Abstract: Large multimodal models tend to hallucinate visual detail fluently, which limits their deployment for fine-grained interpretation. We present HALDETECT, our system for the English hallucination-detection track (Task 1b) of ImageEval 2026, in which a system must identify, from an image and three culturally plausible statements, the single visually grounded one. We frame the item as one contrastive decision, emit the answer before its explanation, and structure reasoning around colour/texture, shape/form, and context. Our best submitted adapter fine-tunes Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct with 4-bit QLoRA while freezing the vision encoder and reaches Contrastive Instability (CI) 0.035 on the 1,000-item test set; we placed third of eight teams. Development experiments show that answer order can matter more than model scale and that adaptation beats prompting alone. Retrospective paired analysis of the released gold labels confirms the QLoRA gain over the best prompt but not the small gap between the devtest-selected and best-test adapters, and reseeding all four training sizes shows that the apparent data-scaling curve does not survive a seed change. The 35 residual errors are culturally plausible function, material, and recognition distinctions; naive adapter voting does not help.

</details>


### [31] [SCINTILLA-SNN: A Spiking Multi-Scale Selective Aggregation Network for Perineural Invasion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11237)
*Youngung Han,Yului Jeong,Kyeonghun Kim,Dohyun Kweon,Suah Park,Hyunsu Go,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Jinyong Jun,Yunho Choe,Yunjin Seo,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SCINTILLA-SNN脉冲神经网络，用于胆管癌神经周围浸润术前MRI预测，5折交叉验证AUROC达0.748，推理能耗降低23.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胆管癌神经周围浸润（PNI）的术前预测具有临床价值，但MRI上PNI相关线索细微、稀疏且局限于肿瘤边界周围。标准3D CNN和Transformer以稠密或空间均匀方式处理体数据，易稀释细微PNI证据，且在3D特征网格上需大量乘累加运算。

Method: 提出SCINTILLA-SNN，由四阶段分层骨干网络和多尺度脉冲聚合（MSSA）模块组成。骨干通过脉冲卷积阶段与局部脉冲窗口调制阶段提取分层体表示；MSSA将每个空间token映射为可学习内容值，并用基于放电率和逐时间步膜电位变异性得到的脉冲动力学门进行调制，生成诊断token分数以选择性聚合稀疏PNI证据。

Result: 在182例胆管癌患者10年回顾性队列上，5折交叉验证AUROC为0.748；与同网络稠密MAC计算相比，估计推理能耗降低23.18倍。

Conclusion: SCINTILLA-SNN通过脉冲计算与稀疏证据选择性聚合，在保持有竞争力的PNI预测性能的同时显著降低能耗，为资源受限场景下的术前预测提供可行方案。

Abstract: Preoperative prediction of perineural invasion (PNI) in cholangiocarcinoma (CCA) is clinically valuable but remains challenging because PNI-related cues on magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) are subtle, sparse, and spatially localized around the tumor boundary. Standard 3D CNN and transformer architectures process volumetric data in a dense or spatially uniform manner, which can dilute subtle PNI-related evidence while requiring a large number of multiply-accumulate operations over 3D feature grids. To address these limitations, we propose SCINTILLA-SNN, a 3D spiking network composed of a four-stage hierarchical backbone and a Multi-Scale Spike Aggregation (MSSA) module for PNI prediction. The backbone extracts hierarchical volumetric representations through spiking convolutional stages and local spike window modulation stages. Given the resulting stage-wise representations, MSSA maps each spatial token to a learnable content value and modulates it with a spike-dynamics gate derived from firing rate and timestep-wise membrane-potential variability. The resulting score, referred to as the diagnostic token score, is used to selectively aggregate sparse PNI-related evidence. Experiments on a 10-year retrospective cohort of 182 CCA patients show that SCINTILLA-SNN achieves an AUROC of 0.748 under 5-fold cross-validation, while reducing the estimated inference energy by 23.18$\times$ compared with dense MAC-only computation of the same network.

</details>


### [32] [Fast and Accurate Monomodal 3D High Resolution Deep Registration of Drosophila Larval Brain Volumes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11240)
*Daniel Reisenbüchler,Yousef Sadegheih,Michael Dittrich,Pratibha Kumari,Muhammad Usman,Dorit Merhof*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出深度配准流水线，将果蝇幼虫脑在单次前向传播中可形变对齐到参考模板，精度、速度与鲁棒性均优于多种经典与学习基线，并开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果蝇幼虫脑是紧凑的神经科学研究模型，其遗传工具可在特定神经群体中表达荧光标记；比较不同个体的表达模式需将所有脑配准到共享解剖参考空间。现有流程多基于经典配准方法，对每个体数据都需重新优化、常需逐例调参、单脑耗时数分钟，难以作为常规预处理步骤。

Method: 提出已训练的深度配准流水线：在单次前向传播中以高空间分辨率将幼虫脑可形变对齐到参考模板，处理的体数据体素数远超通常报道的学习式3D配准规模；同时配套提供预处理流程与以解剖标志锚定的评测流程。

Result: 在包含不同采集条件与质量分层的留出数据集上，与11种经典基线和7种学习式基线对比，该流水线最准确，在解剖标志局部互信息上相对最强经典基线提升23个百分点；配准速度快经典可形变流程1–2个数量级；随采集质量下降，其精度保持能力优于所有其他方法。

Conclusion: 该深度配准框架在精度、速度与质量鲁棒性上均领先，网络、训练权重及完整流程已作为开源框架释放：https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg。

Abstract: The larval stage of Drosophila melanogaster is a compact model system for neuroscience whose genetic toolkit allows fluorescent markers to be expressed in defined neural populations, and comparing the resulting expression patterns across animals requires every brain to be registered into a shared anatomical reference space. Existing pipelines for this task are predominantly based on classical registration methods, which perform a new optimization for each volume, often require per-case parameter tuning, and can take minutes per brain, limiting their use as a routine preprocessing step. We present a trained deep registration pipeline that deformably aligns a larval brain to a reference template in a single forward pass at high spatial resolution, on volumes that hold several times more voxels than those learned 3D registration is normally reported on, together with the preprocessing and anatomy-anchored evaluation pipeline required to apply it. Against eleven classical and seven further learned baselines on a held-out collection acquired with different acquisition and quality strata, the proposed pipeline is the most accurate, improving on the strongest classical baseline by 23 percentage points of anatomical landmark-local mutual information. It registers a volume one to two orders of magnitude faster than the classical deformable pipelines, and it retains more of its accuracy than any other method as acquisition quality degrades. The network, its trained weights and the full pipeline are released as the open-source deep larval brain registration framework: https://github.com/agentdr1/deep-larval-brain-reg

</details>


### [33] [From Evaluation to Enhancement: Benchmarking and Improving Think-with-Video Reasoning for Video Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11242)
*Meng Luo,Yicheng Liu,Jiahao Wang,Yuanxing Zhang,Xin Tao,Pengfei Wan,Kun Gai,Hao Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出VWG-Bench基准（9个推理维度、38个细粒度任务）和三层次VLM-as-Judge评估协议，揭示视频生成模型"渲染强、推理弱"的差距，并提出Vid-PRE提示改写器将推理负担卸载给VLM，显著提升生成器的推理表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型虽然视觉质量高，但能否真正"用视频思考"——执行符号规则、遵守物理规律、追求目标——尚不清楚。已有基准只能部分覆盖该问题，且常将视觉质量与认知正确性混为一谈，缺乏精确的诊断工具。

Method: 1）构建VWG-Bench，覆盖9个推理维度、38个细粒度任务；2）设计三层级VLM-as-Judge协议，分别评估视频级流畅度、任务级规则遵循、样本级目标达成；3）提出Vid-PRE，一个模型无关的提示改写器，将推理认知负担卸载给专用VLM，仅用纯文本奖励进行强化学习训练，产生简洁且约束感知的提示，避免视频级奖励信号的不稳定性。

Result: 对主流模型的评测显示明显差距：渲染分数很高，但在逻辑密集和规则约束任务上持续失败。Vid-PRE在多个生成器上带来显著的推理提升，且无需修改生成器架构。

Conclusion: VWG-Bench提供了严格的诊断视角，Vid-PRE提供了可扩展的改进路径，二者共同推动视频生成模型走向真正的"用视频思考"能力。数据与代码已公开。

Abstract: Video generation has advanced to produce visually compelling and temporally coherent results. Yet, whether these models can genuinely think with video--executing symbolic rules, respecting physical laws, and pursuing intentional goals--remains an open question. Existing benchmarks only partially address this, often conflating visual quality with cognitive correctness. We introduce VWG-Bench (Video World Generalist Benchmark), a comprehensive benchmark spanning 9 reasoning dimensions and 38 fine-grained tasks. To enable precise diagnosis, we design a three-level VLM-as-Judge protocol that independently assesses video-level fluency, task-level rule adherence, and sample-level goal realization. Evaluations of leading models reveal a striking gap: while models achieve strong rendering scores, they consistently fail on logic-heavy and rule-constrained tasks. To address this, we propose Vid-PRE (Video Prompt Reasoner and Enhancer), a model-agnostic prompt rewriter that offloads the cognitive burden of reasoning to a dedicated VLM. Trained via reinforcement learning with purely text-based rewards, Vid-PRE produces concise, constraint-aware prompts without the instability of video-level reward signals. Experiments show that Vid-PRE yields substantial reasoning improvements across multiple generators without architectural modifications. Together, VWG-Bench and Vid-PRE offer a rigorous diagnostic lens and a scalable path toward true think-with-video capabilities. All data and code are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/KlingTeam/VWG-Bench.

</details>


### [34] [Uncertainty DMD: Restoring Diversity in Few-Step Autoregressive Video Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11265)
*Zixuan Duan,Xunzhi Xiang,Yabo Chen,Xin Zhang,Changhan Liu,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Qi Fan,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Uncertainty DMD，通过在自回归视频生成的第一个 chunk 注入时间步扰动、后续 chunk 采用随机 cache 写入，恢复少步蒸馏中的随机性，提升多样性与运动动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步蒸馏虽提升自回归视频生成效率，却导致多样性崩塌：同一提示下不同噪声样本生成高度相似、运动动态减弱的视频。作者分析发现，在自回归设定下这表现为"结构化不确定性崩塌"——DMD 的寻模偏置把不同噪声映射到几乎相同的第一段 chunk，随后确定性 AR cache 将该崩塌状态传播到所有后续 chunk，使根部的局部随机性丧失演变为全局时间变化抑制。

Method: 提出 Uncertainty DMD 不确定性注入框架，在 AR 生成两个关键阶段恢复随机性：（1）对第一个 chunk 施加 timestep 扰动以增加首段多样性；（2）对后续 chunk 引入随机 cache 写入机制，在自回归条件中保留不确定性。方法无需改变网络架构，仅引入轻量扰动操作，且训练与推理使用相同的扰动机制。

Result: 实验表明 Uncertainty DMD 在保持单样本视觉质量相当的前提下，持续提升生成多样性与运动动态。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了 DMD 蒸馏自回归视频生成中"结构化不确定性崩塌"的机理，并给出一种无需架构改动、轻量且训练推理一致的随机性注入方案，有效缓解多样性崩塌。

Abstract: Few-step distillation improves the efficiency of autoregressive (AR) video generation, but often causes diversity collapse: under the same prompt, different noise samples tend to produce highly similar videos with weakened motion dynamics. We analyze this degradation in Distribution Matching Distillation (DMD)-distilled AR video generators and find that, in the autoregressive setting, it takes the form of a structured uncertainty collapse: the mode-seeking bias of DMD maps different noise samples to nearly identical first chunks, and the deterministic AR cache then propagates this collapsed state to all subsequent chunks, turning a local loss of stochasticity at the rollout root into a global suppression of temporal variation. Based on this analysis, we propose Uncertainty DMD, a simple uncertainty-injection framework that restores stochasticity at two key stages of AR generation: a timestep perturbation for the first chunk to increase first-chunk diversity, and a stochastic cache-writing mechanism for later chunks to preserve uncertainty in autoregressive conditioning. The method requires no architectural changes and introduces only lightweight perturbation operations. The same perturbation mechanisms are used during both training and inference. Experiments show that Uncertainty DMD consistently improves diversity and motion dynamics while maintaining comparable per-sample visual quality.

</details>


### [35] [Improving Faint Object Detection for Space Situational Awareness with Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11269)
*Angela Cratere,Luca Ghilardi,Vishnu Reddy,Francesco Dell'Olio,Charalampos S. Kouzinopoulos,Roberto Furfaro*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出深度学习流水线，通过自动去星与背景重建增强光学SSA中暗弱运动目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学观测中低信噪比目标检测困难，尤其在X-GEO环境，结构化天空背景、密集恒星场和散射月光严重削弱经典检测算法性能。

Method: 流水线结合轻量分割网络Tiny-U-Net生成恒星掩膜，以及部分卷积变分自编码器astro-VAE学习天文背景统计分布并对掩膜区域进行语境感知修复；重建背景图作为预处理抑制固定源与背景不均，并与shift-and-stack方案集成。

Result: 在真实地基望远镜X-GEO观测数据上验证，方法高保真重建无星背景，保留运动目标并显著提升可检测性。

Conclusion: 该方法为光学SSA场景中暗弱运动目标检测提供了有效的数据驱动预处理策略。

Abstract: We present a deep-learning pipeline for enhancing the detection of faint moving objects in optical space situational awareness (SSA) imagery through automated star removal and background reconstruction. Detecting low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) objects remains extremely challenging in optical observations, particularly in the cislunar (X-GEO) environment, where structured sky backgrounds, dense stellar fields, and scattered moonlight significantly degrade the performance of classical detection algorithms. To address this problem, the proposed pipeline combines a lightweight segmentation network (Tiny-U-Net) to generate stellar masks with a partial-convolution variational autoencoder (astro-VAE), designed to learn the statistical distribution of astronomical backgrounds and perform context-aware inpainting of masked regions. The reconstructed background maps can then be used as a preprocessing step to suppress fixed sources and background inhomogeneities prior to detection. As a proof of concept, the approach is integrated with a shift-and-stack scheme and evaluated on real ground-based telescope observations targeting the X-GEO region. Results demonstrate that the method reconstructs star-free backgrounds with high fidelity, while preserving moving targets and significantly enhancing detectability, thereby providing an effective data-driven preprocessing strategy for faint moving-object detection in optical SSA scenarios.

</details>


### [36] [Order-Aware 2.5D Multiple Instance Learning for Preoperative MRI-Based Perineural Invasion Risk Assessment in Intrahepatic Cholangiocarcinoma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11271)
*Hyunsu Go,Youngung Han,Kyeonghun Kim,Jinyong Jun,Junbeom Lee,Dohyun Kweon,Yului Jeong,Suah Park,Sungha Park,Anna Jung,Woo Kyoung Jeong,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出顺序感知板层多实例学习（OAS-MIL），在仅有患者级标签、无切片级标注的条件下，从术前T2加权MRI预测肝内胆管癌的神经周围侵犯（PNI），平均AUROC达0.770。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PNI是肝内胆管癌的不良组织病理学标志物，但通常只能通过术后切除标本确认。若能在术前无创地预测PNI，有助于治疗决策。然而现有数据仅有患者级标签，缺乏切片级或体素级标注，难以直接训练定位模型。

Method: 提出OAS-MIL弱监督框架：将肿瘤为中心的MRI裁剪表示为连续轴位切片构成的重叠2.5D板层的有序序列；使用共享编码器提取板层级特征；再分别通过置换不变集合注意力分支和双向序列注意力分支进行聚合。

Result: 采用患者级五折标签分层交叉验证，OAS-MIL平均AUROC为0.770，优于所评估的体数据和MIL基线方法。

Conclusion: 轴位顺序为弱监督MRI的PNI预测提供了有效的归纳偏置，说明顺序感知的多实例学习可提升患者级PNI预测性能。

Abstract: Perineural invasion (PNI) is an adverse histopathologic marker in intrahepatic cholangiocarcinoma (ICC), but it is usually confirmed only after resection. Preoperative T2-weighted MRI may provide noninvasive imaging cues predictive of PNI, although labels are available only at the patient level without slice- or voxel-level annotations. We propose Order-Aware Slab Multiple Instance Learning (OAS-MIL), a weakly supervised framework for patient-level PNI prediction. Each tumor-centered MRI crop is represented as an ordered sequence of overlapping 2.5D slabs formed from contiguous axial slices. A shared encoder extracts slab-level features, which are aggregated by a permutation-invariant set-attention branch and a bidirectional sequence-attention branch. Using five-fold label-stratified cross-validation at the patient level, OAS-MIL achieved a mean AUROC of 0.770, outperforming the evaluated volumetric and MIL baselines. These results suggest that axial order provides a useful inductive bias for weakly supervised PNI prediction from MRI.

</details>


### [37] [SAMV-DUSt3R: Instance-Centric 3D Scene Decoupling from Sparse Multi-Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11279)
*Langxu Zhao,Zuan Gu,Yingdan Zhang,Pengfei Zhao,Tianhan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SAMV-DUSt3R端到端模型，将SAM2的2D掩码注入MV-DUSt3R重建，实现物体级解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D场景中物体解耦需求日益增长，但现有方法多依赖多阶段流水线，缺乏端到端的实例级解耦能力。

Method: 引入Cross Flow Mask Block，利用SAM2掩码引导网络聚焦目标实例，联合提升形状精度；并用轻量级Spatial RankGNN选择最优参考视图以保证重建稳定性。

Result: 参考视图选择准确率达73.5%；相比SOTA基线，各指标平均重建精度提升11%。

Conclusion: 方法展现强实例解耦能力，对自动驾驶、机器人、AR/VR及遗产数字化有明显价值。

Abstract: With the rising demand to decouple objects from 3D scenes, we propose SAMV-DUSt3R, an end-to-end model that injects SAM2 2D masks into MV-DUSt3R reconstruction. A Cross Flow Mask Block uses these masks to steer the network toward the target instance, jointly improving shape accuracy and achieving object-level disentanglement without multi-stage pipelines. To ensure reconstruction stability, a lightweight Spatial RankGNN selects the optimal reference view with a selection accuracy of 73.5\%. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method boosts average reconstruction precision by 11\% across various metrics compared to state-of-the-art baselines. These results reveal a strong instance-disentanglement capability and clear benefits for driving, robotics, AR/VR, and heritage digitisation.

</details>


### [38] [Your Model Already Knows Don't Teach It, Learn to Ask It: Soft Prompting for Few-Shot Adaptation of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11310)
*Gautam Rajendrakumar Gare,Siyi Li,Hewei Wang,Cesar Daniel Hernandez,Wei Zhao,Wolfgang M. Pauli,John Galeotti,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在仅用10张标注图像的跨域少样本目标检测中，软提示（soft prompting）把连续提示token放在视觉-文本交叉模态边界、并用空空格token初始化，仅用1–3个token（约7,168参数）即可匹配最佳LoRA配置的精度，训练参数量少2万倍以上，且不造成预训练能力遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）的跨域适配方法主要是离散提示优化和LoRA微调。作者希望重新审视第三种选择——软提示，在航空、工业、医学等域外场景、仅10张标注图像的少样本检测设定下，探究软提示能否以极少参数达到LoRA的效果，同时避免灾难性遗忘。

Method: 冻结预训练骨干，仅优化少量连续提示token。研究两个关键设计：提示token的放置位置（放在视觉与文本token之间的跨模态边界 vs 其他位置）以及提示初始化方式（用空空格token初始化 vs 语义/随机初始化）。并与LoRA（不同秩）做对比，评估参数效率、方差、遗忘、跨模型迁移性、可言语化（verbalization）以及向机器人操作任务的扩展（冻结的π0.5 VLA策略，token置于梯度瓶颈处）。

Result: 放置于跨模态边界优于其他位置（10.0 vs 8.4 mAP）；空空格初始化优于语义与随机初始化。1–3个可学习token（平均7,168参数）在Roboflow20-VL（14.2 mAP, 10-shot）上匹配最佳LoRA配置，训练参数少20,000倍以上。软提示更难优化、跨随机种子方差更高，但不造成遗忘：匹配精度的LoRA秩使NaturalBench VQA精度相对下降35%（最大秩达56%），而软提示保持预训练性能不变。学习到的token表现为提示而非权重：无需重训即可迁移到新模型（Qwen3.5-9B上+0.8 mAP），并可言语化为可读提示，与提示搜索方法竞争（匹敌DetPO、超过GEPA）。在RoboCasa操作任务上，冻结的π0.5 VLA策略在三个任务中的两个上匹配LoRA基线（token置于梯度瓶颈）。

Conclusion: 现代VLM已编码了专业领域所需的大部分信息，关键挑战在于"如何提问"。软提示是一种参数极省、可迁移、可解释且不损害预训练能力的跨域少样本适配方案，可推广到检测之外的机器人操作任务。

Abstract: We address few-shot object detection with vision-language models (VLMs) in out-of-domain settings such as aerial, industrial, and medical imagery, using only ten annotated images for supervision. Existing adaptation methods are discrete prompt optimization and LoRA fine-tuning. We revisit a third option: soft prompting, where a small number of continuous prompt tokens are optimized while the pretrained backbone remains frozen.
  We identify two key design choices. First, placing prompt tokens at the cross-modal boundary between visual and text tokens outperforms other placements (10.0 vs. 8.4 mAP). Second, initializing prompts from the empty space token outperforms semantic and random initialization.
  With these choices, one to three learned tokens (7,168 parameters on average) match the best LoRA configuration on Roboflow20-VL (14.2 mAP, 10-shot) while training over 20,000x fewer parameters. Soft prompting remains harder to optimize, exhibiting higher variance across random seeds. Unlike LoRA, however, it causes no forgetting: the LoRA rank matching our accuracy reduces NaturalBench VQA accuracy by 35% relative, rising to 56% at the largest rank, whereas soft prompting leaves pretrained performance unchanged.
  The learned tokens behave like prompts rather than weights. They transfer to a newer model without retraining (+0.8 mAP on Qwen3.5-9B) and can be verbalized into readable prompts competitive with prompt-search methods (matching DetPO and outperforming GEPA).
  The approach also extends beyond detection. On RoboCasa manipulation tasks, the frozen $π_{0.5}$ vision-language-action policy benefits from soft prompting, matching the LoRA baseline on two of three tasks when tokens are placed at the gradient bottleneck. These results suggest modern VLMs already encode much of what is needed for specialized domains; the challenge is learning how to ask.

</details>


### [39] [GRIPNet: Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Guided Architecture for Pulmonary Nodule Detection in CT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11312)
*Haojie Yang,Ran Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发现肺结节强度分布具有高斯径向衰减规律，并据此提出GRIPNet检测器，在多个基准上显著提升小结节检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌致死率高，低剂量CT筛查是早期诊断主要途径，而该途径依赖于检出最小的病灶。但小于6毫米的结节仍难以检测，原因是大多数方法将结节视为通用目标，忽略了其成像物理特性所决定的外观规律。

Method: 作者先验证结节外观的高度规律性：强度在几何中心达到峰值并呈高斯径向衰减，对三个公开基准的18,218个标注病灶拟合显示，各数据集和各尺寸层级的平均径向决定系数均高于0.86。由此指出方形卷积均匀采样两轴，与这种径向信号不匹配，对小结节尤为严重。基于此提出GRIPNet，其每个模块都对应强度分布的可测量属性：风车卷积分解径向梯度，双频模块分离边界细节与结构上下文，空洞掩码注意力匹配衰减范围，自适应损失按显著性重加权样本。

Result: GRIPNet在KanserSet、LUNA16和Lung-PET-CT-Dx上将mAP@0.5分别提升至95.3%、91.6%和97.9%，同时以实时速度提升高IoU定位精度。

Conclusion: 结节强度分布的高斯径向规律为检测器设计提供了可解释的物理先验，GRIPNet通过将各模块与可测量属性对应，有效改善了小结节检测与高IoU定位性能，并保持实时速度。

Abstract: Lung cancer causes more deaths than any other malignancy, and low-dose CT screening is the main pathway to early diagnosis. That pathway hinges on the smallest lesions, yet nodules below six millimeters remain hard to detect, because most methods treat a nodule as a generic object and ignore the imaging physics behind its appearance. We show that this appearance is highly regular. Intensity peaks at the geometric center of a nodule and decays radially in a Gaussian pattern, and a fit to 18,218 annotated lesions from three public benchmarks yields a mean radial coefficient of determination above 0.86 in every dataset and size stratum. A square convolution samples both axes uniformly and is mismatched to this radial signal, most severely for small nodules. Guided by this evidence, we propose GRIPNet (Gaussian Radial Intensity Prior Network), a detector in which every module maps to a measurable property of the intensity distribution. Pinwheel convolutions decompose radial gradients, a dual-frequency module separates boundary detail from structural context, dilated masked attention matches the decay extent, and an adaptive loss reweights samples by conspicuity. GRIPNet raises mAP@0.5 to 95.3, 91.6 and 97.9 percent on KanserSet, LUNA16 and Lung-PET-CT-Dx while sharpening high-IoU localization at real-time speed.

</details>


### [40] [Mi-Ripple: Restoring Images Degraded by Iterative AI Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11317)
*Jiayin Chen,Yicheng Xu,Muting Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Mi-Ripple：一种诊断引导的迭代参考条件图像编辑修复流程，分离并抑制周期性网格伪影与颗粒状纹理，在保护结构的同时减少数字波纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代参考条件图像编辑会引入网格状与颗粒状纹理（数字波纹），损害生成图像的视觉质量，需要一种能在抑制伪影的同时保护图像结构的修复方法。

Method: Mi-Ripple将周期性晶格伪影与内容纠缠的颗粒纹理分离，结合选择性频谱陷波、结构感知平滑以及清洁参考再生；当伪影频谱孤立时采用低失真滤波，当滤波会抹除合法细节时采用视觉重建。

Result: 在十四次仅陷波执行中，全图残差标准差为0.08–0.44 CIELAB亮度单位；在成对再生示例中，参考清洁使输出碎屑密度降低45%。

Conclusion: Mi-Ripple将可测量的伪影减少与视觉上更干净的生成图像联系起来，而非仅优化频谱指标。

Abstract: Iterative reference-conditioned image editing can introduce grid-like and granular textures, commonly described as digital ripple. We present Mi-Ripple, a diagnosis-guided restoration workflow that suppresses this digital ripple while protecting image structure. Mi-Ripple separates periodic lattice artifacts from content-entangled granular texture, then combines selective spectral notching, structure-aware smoothing, and cleaned-reference regeneration. This separation enables low-distortion filtering when artifacts are spectrally isolated and visual reconstruction when filtering would erase legitimate detail. Across fourteen notch-only executions, whole-image residual standard deviation is 0.08--0.44 in CIELAB lightness units. In a paired regeneration example, reference cleaning reduces output debris density by 45\%. Mi-Ripple links measurable artifact reduction to visibly cleaner generated images, rather than optimizing a spectral score alone.

</details>


### [41] [Predictive Multi-Landmark OCT Tracking for Increased Motion Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11330)
*Konrad Reuter,Suresh Guttikonda,Chaitali Uday Karekar,Christian Betz,Alexander Schlaefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种预测性追踪方法，通过在多个被追踪地标之间传播位置更新以获得全局位姿预测，从而提升OCT在高速度下的追踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学相干断层扫描（OCT）因高空间分辨率和固有深度感知能力，在无标记运动追踪方面具有潜力。但现有基于OCT的追踪方法可追踪速度有限，尤其是当为进行6D位姿估计而顺序追踪多个地标时，速度限制更为明显。

Method: 提出一种预测性追踪方法：在多个被追踪地标之间传播位置更新，进而获得全局位姿预测。通过全局运动传播与预测，改善高速条件下的追踪表现。

Result: 在速度高达100 mm/s、连续追踪最多九个地标的条件下，RMSE低于1 mm。

Conclusion: 全局运动传播与预测能够有效提升基于OCT追踪的鲁棒性，具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Optical coherence tomography is a promising modality for markerless motion tracking due to its high spatial resolution and inherent depth perception. However, existing OCT-based tracking approaches are limited in terms of trackable velocity, particularly when multiple landmarks are tracked sequentially for 6D pose estimation. In this work, we present a predictive tracking approach that propagates positional updates between multiple tracked landmarks to obtain a global pose prediction. This enables more robust tracking under high velocities. Our results demonstrate RMSEs below 1 mm for velocities up to 100 mm/s and up to nine consecutively tracked landmarks, highlighting the potential of global motion propagation and prediction for improving the robustness of OCT-based tracking.

</details>


### [42] [R4Tun: LLM-guided adaptive segmental tunnel lining segmentation in point clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11360)
*Xinghui Tao,Zehao Ye,Guangming Wang,Jelena Ninić,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出R4Tun，一个由大语言模型驱动的自适应框架，在专家设计的SAM4Tun流水线上进行有界参数调节，利用记忆、状态、知识三种结构化上下文，提升三维点云隧道衬砌分割的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化检测分割式隧道衬砌需要从三维点云中进行自适应分割，但由专家手动调参的流水线在隧道条件变化时性能会显著下降，缺乏适应性。

Method: 提出R4Tun，基于LLM的适应框架，扩展专家设计的SAM4Tun流水线，通过记忆(m)、状态(s)、知识(k)三种结构化上下文引导有界参数调节；在30个Seg2Tunnel子集（13个规则、17个复杂）和3种LLM上评估，共270次运行（30隧道×3模型×3上下文设置）。

Result: 完整的m+s+k设计将mIoU从0.18提升至0.43–0.48，整体准确率从0.42提升至0.59–0.65；接近参考的规则（交错）子集mIoU达到0.784–0.796。不同LLM呈现相似的参数调整趋势（均值增益95%置信区间重叠），并一致调整一组共享的关键参数。

Conclusion: R4Tun在测试的SAM4Tun–Seg2Tunnel场景中是一种可控、免标注、跨LLM的适应机制，能带来一致准确率增益；其定位为机制性贡献而非可部署的最终检测系统，每次有界参数调整均可通过日志化理由进行审计。

Abstract: Automated inspection of segmental tunnel linings requires adaptive segmentation from 3D point clouds, yet expert-tuned pipelines often degrade when tunnel conditions vary. This paper presents R4Tun, a large language model (LLM)-driven adaptation framework that extends an expert-designed pipeline (SAM4Tun) with bounded parameter tuning informed by structured context: memory ($m$), state ($s$), and knowledge ($k$). Evaluated on 30 selected Seg2Tunnel subsets (13 regular, 17 complex) across three LLMs, the full $m+s+k$ design raised mean Intersection-over-Union (mIoU) from 0.18 to 0.43--0.48 and overall accuracy (OA) from 0.42 to 0.59--0.65 relative to the static SAM4Tun baseline, with the near-reference regular (staggered) subsets reaching mIoU 0.784--0.796 across LLMs. Across 270 (30 tunnels $\times$ 3 different LLMs $\times$ 3 context settings) runs, the LLMs showed similar parameter-adjustment trends (with overlapping 95\% CIs on mean gains) and consistently adjusted a shared set of critical parameters. These results support R4Tun as a controlled, label-free, cross-LLM adaptation mechanism in the tested SAM4Tun--Seg2Tunnel setting, demonstrating consistent accuracy gains; we position R4Tun as a mechanism contribution rather than a deployable final-inspection system, in which each bounded parameter change is auditable via logged rationales.

</details>
