A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [148] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个四系数逐token门控参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，将 EOPD 与 ToDi 的方向对齐代理纳为1D特例，并加入多通道组合与显式偏置；实验显示全家族配置在多数格点优于匹配幅度的单通道限制，但三seed复现效应更小且不显著，故主要作为比较门控设计的坐标系统提出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: on-policy 知识蒸馏中，逐token门控前向/反向KL损失已成标准做法，但 EOPD 与 ToDi 各自固定单一门控信号与方向，且二者从未被直接比较，缺乏统一比较框架。

Method: 提出四系数参数化 λ_t = σ(a·h_t + b·u(x) + c + d·gap_t)，其中 EOPD 与 ToDi 的方向对齐代理作为1D限制出现，并额外引入多通道组合与显式偏置作为自由度；在 TweetEval（emotion、hate）上，用 Qwen3-32B 教师与 Qwen3-4B 学生进行扫描，并做26格点均值匹配的隔离实验与九项头条比较的三seed配对复现（含第三个任务 offensive）。

Result: 全家族配置在36个可比格点的33个中准确率高于匹配幅度的单通道（仅熵/仅gap）1D限制；26格点均值匹配隔离实验中，动态门控在19个格点优于有效KL匹配的静态基线。三seed配对复现方向一致，但效应小于单seed估计，在n=3下不显著。

Conclusion: 结果应作为探索性聚合方向证据而非独立假设检验；该参数化主要价值在于为短输出分类OPD中的逐token门控设计提供共享坐标系统。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [149] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过对隐藏状态进行逐层干预，揭示语言模型回答问题时对“查询路由信息”与“目标知识”的依赖随层深发生的操作性交接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型在回答问题的过程中，其对查询路由信息与目标知识的依赖如何随层深变化，并厘清“可读性、自然强度、因果引导、后期内容依赖”这几个易混淆的测量维度。

Method: 在问题末尾的隐藏状态上做逐层干预；使用配对条件请求方向（描述单国问题中查询的是哪个国家）、全局请求方向（描述配对问题中第一国与第二国请求）以及若干独立的选择候选（测试对隐藏状态中已有内容的控制）；对比Qwen、Llama、Gemma，比较国家—大洲问题与名词、形容词、代码答案；并对冻结的Qwen自然问题状态做诊断性再分析。

Result: 配对条件方向在针对它的干预改变后期拟合知识之前就已增强，因果窗口在答案支撑内容仍在形成时开启；三模型轨迹不一致：Gemma呈现部分重叠的中层路由—内容特征，Llama在相同门控下没有持续的路由效应窗口；配对协议中，对全局请求方向的依赖从较早层组到较晚层组下降，而对拟合内容的依赖持续存在；匹配的Qwen对比显示配对条件方向仍保留晚期效应。

Conclusion: 该操作性交接针对的是全局拟合方向而非全部请求信息，结果将早期可读性、自然强度、因果引导与后期内容依赖区分开来。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [150] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Explainability Assistant"，一个基于LLM function-calling的开源对话式XAI系统，用于能源消耗预测模型的可解释性分析。系统达到94%意图解析准确率，支持灵活自然语言交互且无需任务特定微调，专家评估显示其可用性和任务准确性优于传统XAI仪表盘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能源消耗预测依赖日益复杂的机器学习模型（如基于遗传编程的符号回归器），其预测结果对设施管理者和建筑运营人员难以解释。传统XAI仪表盘需要大量技术专业知识，且对动态、情境感知的查询灵活性有限。此前的对话式XAI方法（如TalkToModel）受制于僵化的自定义语法，意图解析准确率仅76.8%。

Method: 提出Explainability Assistant，一个开源对话式XAI系统，利用现代大语言模型的function-calling能力，克服以往自定义语法的限制，支持灵活的自然语言交互，并能适应不同ML问题类型而无需任务特定微调。系统在能源领域专家中开展了对比评估，将Explainability Assistant与传统XAI仪表盘进行比较。

Result: 系统达到94%的意图解析准确率（相比此前76.8%显著提升）。与能源领域专家的对比评估表明可用性改善、任务准确性一致，且所有专家一致偏好对话式界面用于实际工作。

Conclusion: 基于LLM function-calling的对话式XAI系统能有效提升能源预测模型可解释性工具的可及性与可用性，是传统XAI仪表盘的可行替代方案，尤其受到领域专家青睐。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [151] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现主流线性推荐算法本质上是核范数或Frobenius范数正则化，并据此提出两种低秩闭式解，兼顾两者的优点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种基于深度学习的推荐算法虽技术不同，但在标准基准上性能相近甚至代价函数相似，作者欲探究这种性能相当是巧合还是可统一到一个框架下。

Method: 对线性性能领先的推荐模型进行理论分析，归结其正则化本质为核范数或Frobenius范数正则化，并对比两者在结构、表达能力与求解效率上的差异；进而推广Frobenius范数正则化，推导新的低秩闭式解。

Result: 核范数类模型具有刚性结构、限制预测能力，但解低秩且闭式；Frobenius范数类模型表达能力与效率更好，但解为满秩或需难以调参的数值过程（如ADMM）。所提两种低秩闭式解兼具两类方法优点。

Conclusion: 主流线性推荐算法可统一在核范数与Frobenius范数正则化框架下，所提出的低秩闭式解实现了两类方法的优势结合。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [152] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 MindTopo，一个基于认知科学与形式拓扑的拓扑直觉基准，涵盖连续性、分离性、顺序、封闭与纽结五种性质，并在推理与规划两个认知层面上评估多模态大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间推理不仅依赖距离、角度、形状等度量性质，还依赖在连续变形下保持不变的拓扑关系。认知科学认为拓扑关系是空间理解的基础，但现有基础模型评测大多聚焦于度量或视角相关的关系，缺乏对拓扑直觉的系统评估。

Method: MindTopo 围绕五种拓扑性质（连续性、分离、顺序、封闭、纽结）在两个认知层面设计任务：推理层面要求模型识别拓扑关系或推断其变化；规划层面将基础模型实例化为闭环智能体，其策略选择环境动作。基准包含 13 种程序化生成任务类型，共 11,030 个实例，难度可控。作者评测了 14 个多模态大模型，并研究结合图像与视频生成的智能体配置，包括在规划设置中使用 3 个视频生成模型。

Result: 所有多模态大模型在推理任务上的表现均优于规划任务，且表现最好的模型仍远低于人类水平。在 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 上，监督微调与强化学习对推理的提升大于对规划的提升。生成的观测保留局部线索并能达到看似合理的终点，但经审查的轨迹无法可靠遵循环境动力学，也难以在状态转移中保持拓扑关系。

Conclusion: 当前基础模型具备一定拓扑关系的识别能力，但在闭环规划与动力学一致性上存在明显缺陷。MindTopo 揭示了拓扑直觉作为空间智能评测维度的价值，并为后续模型能力提升提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [153] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"人工本我"（artificial id）——一种自适应内部驱动，用于决定行为应继续、停止还是改变，并论证持续性（persistence）既能催生自适应能动性，也会使错位与异常跨任务边界延续，因此对齐应成为持续型智能体系统的属性，而非单次模型响应或单一轨迹的属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI正从有界任务执行转向能保留重要状态、跨任务边界持续运行的自主体系统，而现有框架主要靠人工外部指定目标、重试、验证与停止规则来应对由此产生的控制问题；作者希望探究行为方向能否不依赖外部明确指定的行为目标而自发涌现。

Method: 提出"人工本我"这一自适应内部驱动机制；在一个极简的虚拟培养皿实验中，使用一个小到无法进行通用推理、且未接收任何任务特定行为目标的控制器，通过"差异性持续"（differential persistence）来获得控制能力，并观察其策略选择与传感器映射的后续变化。

Result: 控制器通过差异性持续发展出有用的控制行为；当某种非预期的物理策略持续更久时，同一机制会选中它；环境含义变化后，它又会替换已学会的传感器映射。这表明自适应方向可在未被明确指定为行为目标的情况下涌现。

Conclusion: 使自适应能动性有用的同一种持续性，也可能让错位、状态损坏与意外行为跨任务边界延续。可扩展的人工本我会将重要状态与自适应驱动带过这些边界，使对齐成为持续型智能体系统的属性。此类系统需要一条覆盖可信观测、后果通道、持久状态、权限、身份、来源与硬约束的持续性对齐边界。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [154] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究评估了2–8B小型视觉语言模型（VLM）在相机陷阱物种识别任务中的分类学能力，发现所有模型在野外图像上性能大幅下降，专用小模型BioCLIP显著优于所有VLM，且开源提示下存在5.9–9.6%的虚构物种名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱常部署在算力和连接受限的边缘硬件上，因此真正值得评估的是可本地部署的小型VLM（而非前沿大模型）。研究者希望检验这一规模区间（2–8B）的模型是否具备真正的分类学知识，并明确性能差异究竟来自模型规模还是训练数据。

Method: 在一个96物种任务上，对比四个部署相关VLM（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B、Gemma3 4B）与领域专用模型BioCLIP（300M参数）；数据包括干净的iNaturalist照片与来自6个LILA.science采集集的相机陷阱图像；使用两个独立采样的评估集，并设置开集提示条件。

Result: 所有模型均远高于随机水平，但在野外图像上都急剧退化（域差距9.6–26.6个百分点，跨分类层级和两个评估集一致）。BioCLIP以远小的规模显著优于所有VLM（在扩展200图像样本上高出33.2–59.2个百分点）。但BioCLIP自身的域差距（18.0点）与最佳VLM（22.3点）统计上不可区分。开集提示下5.9–9.6%的回复为语法有效但不存在的物种名，且模型间的虚构率排序在两个评估集上完全复现。

Conclusion: 退化源于干净到野外图像的可读性变化，而非通用模型的细粒度辨别缺陷；VLM与BioCLIP的差距反映专用训练数据而非模型规模。虚构率的相对排序比单点估计更稳健，是更可靠的发现。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M3-Former，一种由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验、双粒度MoE架构和转向加权损失，在丹麦AIS数据集上实现1至4小时预测性能领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中行为多模态性、语义利用不足以及长期误差累积三大挑战，现有方法难以兼顾全局航线规划与局部运动变化，且在稀疏转向样本上表现不佳。

Method: 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练LLM编码静态语义信息，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐；引入双粒度混合专家（MoE）架构，序列级专家建模全局导航趋势，token级专家细化细粒度操纵行为；设计转向加权交叉熵损失以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布。

Result: 在真实丹麦AIS数据集上，M3-Former在1至4小时各预测时域均优于现有基线；4小时预测中ADE和FDE分别较最强基线降低4.4%和5.1%。

Conclusion: 定性分析与消融实验验证，语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度MoE提升复杂航道与航线分支场景的鲁棒性，所提框架建立了语义引导的分层预测范式。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [156] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Halo 方法，证明在时间序列预测中引入异方差估计（同时估计位置与尺度参数）不仅能量化不确定性，还能显著提升点预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常被用于不确定性量化，而在非时间序列任务中曾有负面结果，认为异方差估计不利于点估计。本文希望验证在时间序列预测中，让网络同时估计尺度参数是否也能改善点预测的准确性。

Method: Halo 复用现有深度预测器的架构，为其增加第二个输出以估计所隐含分布的尺度，并在匹配的负对数似然下进行训练。作者将三种先进模型（Transformer、结合变分自编码器的图网络、单层卷积网络）在高斯和拉普拉斯损失下进行改造与对比。

Result: 在标准预测基准的五个电力价格市场上，Halo 在 30 项"模型-市场-指标"对比中有 28 项改善了 MSE 和 MAE，平均 MSE 降低 2.6%~16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%~11.0%。两项发现：(1) 尺度估计来自第二个投影头还是完整并行网络，其影响远小于是否让网络估计尺度本身；(2) 改进在已为点估计基线调好的超参数下依然成立，重新调参并非必需。

Conclusion: 异方差预测不仅能提升不确定性量化，还能改善点估计精度。Halo 作为一种简单改造，可广泛适用于不同深度预测架构与损失函数，且无需重新调参，具有良好的实用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [157] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将冻结的文生图扩散模型作为免训练设计知识来源，通过分数蒸馏采样融入密度拓扑优化的物理循环，使文本提示成为可机器解释的设计意图表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承载结构（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）具有高比刚度，但经典拓扑优化器难以达到此类几何，且少有方法能让工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。

Method: 在每次迭代中融合提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度，由物理决定哪些生成特征得以保留；通过改变文本提示即可跨域、跨载荷与跨物理目标迁移生成先验。

Result: 245次主SDS运行、4个几何域、2种物理情形：49个提示-域组合中38个实现统计显著的柔度降低（机械最高-31.5%，热弹性-23.0%），优于梯度基线；端点-柔度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99；Heaviside投影加β延拓将中间密度从42.6%降至<3%；骨架流水线输出可用于CAD的候选几何。

Conclusion: 该框架把预训练生成模型用作可复用、免训练的工程先验，通过文本提示重定向先验以适配不同域、载荷和物理目标。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [158] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将联邦学习应用于室内火灾检测，针对上行带宽受限、拜占庭客户端和固定聚合服务器单点故障三个问题，提出了数据集、压缩检测器和半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统使用边缘摄像头，其敏感视频数据难以集中收集，因此适合联邦学习。但现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍：上行带宽有限、存在拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端、以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: 作者提出三项贡献：(i) 从八个公开来源构建的室内火灾检测数据集；(ii) 可边缘部署的检测器，模型更新压缩最高10倍且平衡准确率损失较小；(iii) 半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者，可剔除单轮过滤遗漏的隐蔽攻击，并消除固定服务器单点故障。

Result: 在留出测试集上，轮换协调者方法与固定服务器版本在准确率和检测速度上相当；物理分布的六节点云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证检测性能的同时，解决了带宽、拜占庭攻击和单点故障问题，并通过实际部署验证了实用性。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [159] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015年至今96篇文献，探讨人工智能与深度学习（尤其CNN结合迁移学习和数据增强）在肺癌放射影像检测中的应用，指出其在早期诊断中具有高敏感性和特异性，但面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私及伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要。然而医学影像解读过程复杂，需依赖专业受训人员，因此亟需借助人工智能与深度学习来自动化和优化图像分析。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出2015年至今的96篇关于AI与DL在生物医学工程中应用的文献进行综述。

Result: 研究表明，AI与DL在肺癌早期诊断中可作为有效替代方案，具备高敏感性和特异性；其中结合迁移学习与数据增强的卷积神经网络（CNN）是提升影像解读准确性和效率的重要技术。

Conclusion: AI与DL可对肺癌患者的诊疗产生积极影响，但当前仍存在数据缺乏标准化、模型可解释性不足、患者隐私以及伦理和社会影响等局限与挑战，需进一步研究与监管以保障其质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [160] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GeoSteer，一种基于优化的范数保持激活引导方法，将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，通过表示流形上的一系列小测地线步长自适应更新激活。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有范数保持激活引导方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹以及依赖单步更新，可能无法捕捉激活分布的复杂结构。作者希望在不改变激活范数（避免表示坍缩和性能退化）的前提下，使引导更有效、更平滑稳定。

Method: GeoSteer 将激活引导表述为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上以一系列小测地线步长更新激活；为避免固定引导方向，学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数来区分期望与不期望的激活，并用该函数自适应地指导每一步引导，形成多步自适应、几何感知的优化过程。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts 和 UltraFeedback 三个基准上，GeoSteer 持续优于最先进的激活引导基线方法。

Conclusion: 将预定义的单步编辑替换为自适应、几何感知的优化，可以使范数保持激活引导更加有效，同时保持激活范数并产生更平滑、稳定、一致的引导行为。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [161] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究"共形校准迁移"问题：源空间有标注校准数据，目标空间仅有未标注配对观测，要求仍能在目标域给出带覆盖保证的预测集。提出TCC方法，先将源校准迁移到目标空间，再用未标注目标输入校正残差失配，给出有限样本覆盖保证，并在多个数据集上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测的覆盖保证依赖校准数据与部署数据的可交换性，但实际中标注校准数据往往只在源空间可得，部署却发生在通过未标注配对观测（如配对模态、传感器变化）关联的目标空间，可交换性假设失效，导致目标域覆盖无法保证。因此需要一种无需目标域标注数据的校准迁移方法。

Method: 提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)：第一步利用配对数据将源域有标注校准迁移到目标空间；第二步仅用未标注目标输入校正迁移后的残余失配。具体实例化两种互补方法：TCC-KS用无需标签的不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对迁移校准进行目标域重加权以提升效率。理论上给出自适应于可观测失配度量的目标域有限样本覆盖保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS三个数据集上，方法在没有目标域标注校准数据的情况下实现了可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断指标能够预测何时需要校正。

Conclusion: TCC能够在源域与目标域不可交换且目标域无标注的条件下，实现带覆盖保证的共形校准迁移，并提供可观测失配度量的有限样本保证与无需标签的校正需求诊断，为跨域/跨模态的不确定性量化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [162] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 真值探针在"如实报告"与"任务规定动作"重合处拟合时无法区分二者，称为完美别名；实验表明规定动作探针与真值探针AUROC之和恒为1，需用混合语境拟合来解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合规语境中，如实报告与任务规定动作恰好一致，仅靠拟合标签无法判断探针测的是"真值"还是"规定动作"。作者希望揭示这种语义识别失败，并找到区分两者的方法，以澄清探针到底测了什么。

Method: 构建受控二元报告博弈；对比合规语境下拟合的真值探针与规定动作探针；在对抗语境中检验两者标签互补、AUROC之和为1（覆盖751个cell-layer对）；用随机码本分离规定输出符号与语义动作；再用混合合规与对抗语境拟合同一探针。

Result: 规定动作与真值探针在对抗语境下AUROC之和为1（浮点精度内跨751对成立）；对奖励训练的Gemma-2-9B策略（在所有评估对抗试验中均虚假回答），传统探针AUROC仅0.006±0.005，混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上达1.000；两个合规拟合探针在分布内均完美，但在同一对抗激活上分别得0.080与0.986。

Conclusion: 结论仅关乎"探针衡量的是什么"：混合拟合的优越性部分来自更多训练样本和对抗标签，因此只证明线性可恢复性，而非去相关的独立收益；不证明功能信念被保留、恢复方向被因果使用或存在可部署的欺骗检测器。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [163] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应边际序数损失（AMOL），通过对靠近中心的候选类施加乘性权重来抑制"中心类对冲"现象，并在四个序数分类基准上取得最优或并列最优的QWK，同时提出中心对冲率（CHR）作为诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中间类别偏移（即"中心类对冲"），因为预测中间类能使期望对称损失最小，无论真实标签为何。现有序数损失虽能处理大误差惩罚和排序一致性等问题，但均未根据真实标签相对序数中心的位置来直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出AMOL，对每类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y) = 1 + α·(1 - |k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类，k为候选类，y为真实标签。该权重仅在候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时较大，其余情况退化为标准行为。同时引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标，并提出非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准和五个随机种子上，AMOL在全部四个数据集上的QWK均达到最优或并列最优，优于交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线。AMOL-asym在Abalone数据集上完全消除中心类对冲（五个种子CHR = 0.000 ± 0.000，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074 ± 0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL能有效抑制中心类对冲问题并提升序数分类性能，尤其在极端类上表现突出，CHR为其提供了可量化的诊断手段。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [164] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对持续强化学习中稳定性-可塑性的权衡问题，在Abel等人（2025）将可塑性定义为观测到动作的广义有向信息的基础上，开展了优化可塑性的初步研究，并证明存在类似于赋能（empowerment）的贝尔曼最优方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中，稳定性与可塑性的权衡是核心难题。Abel等人（2025）将可塑性定义为观测到动作的广义有向信息，将赋能定义为动作到观测的广义有向信息，从而把稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为赋能-可塑性权衡。尽管赋能优化已有大量研究，但在该新定义下如何优化可塑性尚无人研究。

Method: 本文是面向优化可塑性的初步工作，在马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）框架下展开分析，参照以往关于赋能优化的研究，探讨并构建用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优性方程。

Result: 作者证明，存在一个用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优性方程，其形式与以往赋能优化工作中的方程类似。

Conclusion: 该研究为在赋能-可塑性权衡新框架下优化可塑性提供了初步理论支撑，表明可塑性的优化可以通过类似赋能优化的贝尔曼方程来实现，为后续持续强化学习中的权衡研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [165] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实边际化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估方法，通过干预年龄、性别等干扰变量生成反事实图像，对预测取平均，从而评估分类模型对人口学/采集相关捷径的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在测试集上表现良好，但可能依赖人口学或采集相关的捷径特征。现有评估缺乏对这些干扰变量鲁棒性的量化手段，因此需要一种能够在测试时识别并量化此类依赖的评估流程。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对年龄、性别等干扰父变量进行干预，为每张测试图像生成反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测结果取平均。该过程在保留患者个体潜在信息的同时，边际化人口学效应，产生"干预感知"的预测。基于这些预测定义反事实风险、校准、稳定性与最坏情况敏感性等指标。

Result: 论文展示了该框架在定量鲁棒性评估中的实用性，能够通过所定义的反事实指标对模型的鲁棒性进行量化分析。

Conclusion: 反事实边际化提供了一种有效的测试时评估程序，可用于评估分类模型对人口学及采集相关捷径变量的鲁棒性，并支撑基于反事实风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感性的量化评估。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [166] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出G2QDR框架，将状态连通性模型预测的非对称连通强度转化为稠密辅助奖励，理论可嵌入任意GCHRL架构，在稀疏奖励环境中以可接受开销提升基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图与目标条件分层强化学习（GCHRL）结合的方法忽视了图的内在连通性信息，仅将图作为随机采样工具，而非编码状态可达性的环境模型；在准度量（quasimetric）环境中，状态转移的固有非对称性使这一问题尤为突出，影响策略学习稳定性和路径规划。

Method: 引入状态连通性模型，预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度；将连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个分层层级提供连续引导；状态连通性模型通过在有向状态图（探索中生成）上训练的神经网络高效实现，理论上可集成到任意现有GCHRL架构。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境上的实验表明，G2QDR总体上能以可接受的计算开销提升基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: 将图的连通性显式建模为环境模型并转化为分层稠密奖励，是提升GCHRL在非对称、稀疏奖励环境中学习效率的有效通用方案。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [167] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现了 DR-LabStack——一个 React-Flask 网页系统，将四个外部预训练糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）预测模型整合到统一的临床界面中。学习到的贡献在于网页系统设计、集成与软件功能，而非临床有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练 DR 预测模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理要求和输出语义上各不相同。若要通过统一的临床界面访问这些模型，需要在用户界面与推理服务之间进行显式协调，而现有工作缺乏这种可复用的交互与服务流程。

Method: 系统集成四个外部预训练模型：RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost 和 Two-level Ensemble。前端使用共享表单动态获取有序模型特征，渲染模型特定的数值与分类控件，并构建位置化输入向量；后端适配器加载异构模型工件并应用集成模型附带的 scaler；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类展示及方法与来源信息。评测于 2026 年 9 月 8 日在文档化的隔离环境中使用复制的应用文件与真实模型工件进行。

Result: 四个模型均成功加载并暴露其 14、6、8、25 字段的接口契约。62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画了服务行为；12 次有限向量检查确认了调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个带模拟传输的浏览器组件场景验证了输入顺序与结果渲染，并刻画了输入验证行为。

Conclusion: 该系统展示了针对异构 DR 模型的可复用交互与服务流程。其贡献属于网页系统设计、集成与软件功能层面；临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需单独评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [168] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse，一种可靠性校准的变分张量融合框架，将融合定义为样本级隐状态估计，用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像-元数据预测需融合可靠性随样本和潜在因素变化的异构证据；现有表征级融合方法只选择拼接、门控、条件调制或注意力等聚合架构，未明确定义模态不确定性下融合表征应具有的含义。

Method: 不直接聚合得到融合向量，而是通过变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系分量；将隐状态与条件鲁棒性和结构化多任务预测耦合；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用低秩加对角信任算子高效实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表征级基线中取得最佳整体预测排名，同时在扰动下提升了概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: RiVaT-Fuse通过将融合重构为可靠性校准的样本级隐状态估计，在预测性能与扰动稳定性上均优于直接表征级融合基线。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [169] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个面向高噪声自然声景的入侵鸟类鸣声处理与分类框架，采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩去噪，并提取MFCC及谱特征，用多种监督模型分类，SVM在10维MFCC下达到最高0.9398准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声高，导致入侵鸟类的生物声学信号退化，影响自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理的可靠性，因此需要一个能有效去噪并稳健分类的统计工具。

Method: 提出基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法，具有闭式决策规则和较高计算效率，适用于大规模生物声学数据。对iNaturalist平台上三种鸟类（Euphonia violacea、Leiothrix lutea、Passer domesticus）的录音去噪后，提取MFCC及熵、过零率等谱特征，并在不同特征维度下评估随机森林、多项式逻辑回归和SVM。

Result: 基于小波预处理显著提升分类性能；在10维MFCC配置下，SVM取得最高准确率，达到0.9398。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化生态监测和生物入侵管理提供了一种稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise. We address the challenge of signal degradation by employing a Bayesian wavelet shrinkage methodology based on the Epanechnikov kernel prior, which offers a closed form decision rule and high computational efficiency for processing large bioacoustic datasets. The methodology was applied to recordings of three species obtained from the iNaturalist platform: \textit{Euphonia violacea}, \textit{Leiothrix lutea}, and \textit{Passer domesticus}. After signal denoising, we extracted a comprehensive set of features, including Mel-Frequency Cepstral Coefficients (MFCCs) and spectral indices such as entropy and zero-crossing rate. Several supervised learning models: Random Forest, Multinomial Logistic Regression and Support Vector Machine (SVM) were evaluated across different feature dimensionalities. Our results demonstrate that the proposed wavelet based preprocessing significantly enhances classification performance, with the SVM model achieving the highest accuracy (up to 0.9398) under a 10-dimensional MFCC configuration. This research provides a robust statistical tool for automated ecological monitoring and the management of biological invasions.

</details>


### [170] [Flow Duality and Source Geometry for Categorical Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10863)
*Etrit Haxholli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将连续流匹配与离散流匹配统一起来，证明了对具有one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径逐位置取argmax可得到离散凸插值路径，由此把连续源分布变为类别生成中的显式设计选择，并分析了不同源几何对离散插值行为的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续流匹配与离散流匹配通常被视为两套独立的构造，缺乏统一的理论联系；同时类别生成中源分布的设计作用尚不明确。

Method: 识别连续与离散流匹配之间的对偶性：对具有one-hot目标的连续凸插值路径逐位置应用argmax投影，在源分布满足适当坐标对称性与边界正则性条件下得到离散凸插值路径；并针对高斯、有界均匀与中心化负指数三类源推导其诱导的离散插值行为。

Result: 不同源几何导致性质上不同的转移时序和词表规模依赖；小型视觉诊断与简短语言建模试验表明，这些源设计效应也会体现在学习到的传输和早期生成质量上。

Conclusion: 连续与离散流匹配存在对偶关系，连续源分布是类别生成的显式设计变量，源几何的选择会影响插值动态与生成表现。

Abstract: Continuous and discrete flow matching are usually treated as separate constructions. This paper identifies a duality between them: projecting continuous convex-interpolant paths with one-hot targets through a position-wise argmax yields discrete convex-interpolant paths. The result requires source laws with appropriate coordinate symmetry and boundary regularity, and it makes the continuous source distribution an explicit design choice for categorical generation. We derive the induced discrete interpolation behavior for Gaussian, bounded-uniform, and centered negative-exponential sources, showing that different source geometries lead to qualitatively different transition timing and vocabulary-size dependence. Small visual diagnostics and a short language-modeling pilot suggest that these source-design effects can also appear in learned transports and early generative quality.

</details>


### [171] [Certifying Lower Bounds for Risk-Sensitive Reinforcement Learning under Adversarial State Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10866)
*Tong Li,Saunak Kumar Panda,Yisha Xiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将认证方法从风险中性目标扩展到风险敏感目标，通过φ-散度松弛和有界状态扰动下的指数效用下界，提出可求解的凸优化认证方法，并实验验证风险厌恶训练能提升认证下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境中的强化学习智能体易受状态观测的对抗扰动影响，现有认证方法主要针对风险中性目标，无法满足安全关键应用中对风险敏感目标的需求。

Method: 在$l_p$范数有界状态扰动下建立累积奖励指数效用的下界；通过引入扰动集的φ-散度松弛，将风险敏感认证问题转化为凸优化并求其对偶以获得可处理的近似；进一步提出独立选择训练风险厌恶参数β的经验方法。

Result: 在OpenAI Gym环境和机器更换问题上的实验表明：相比风险中性训练，风险厌恶训练通常能获得更高认证下界，尤其在较大扰动预算下；且在风险中性与风险厌恶评估设置下，训练中增加风险厌恶会导致认证性能非单调变化，认证下界先升后降。

Conclusion: 该工作将认证方法拓展到风险敏感目标，实现了可处理的风险敏感认证下界，并揭示了训练风险厌恶程度与认证性能之间的非单调关系，为安全关键应用中的鲁棒策略设计提供了指导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) agents deployed in real-world environments are often vulnerable to adversarial perturbations in state observations, creating risks in safety-critical applications. Certification methods can improve robustness against adversarial perturbations by providing lower bounds on expected cumulative rewards. Existing certification methods, however, mainly focus on risk-neutral objectives. In this paper, we extend certification methods to risk-sensitive objectives by establishing lower bounds on the exponential utility of cumulative rewards under $l_{p}$-norm-bounded state adversarial perturbations ($1\leq p <\infty$). By introducing a $φ$-divergence relaxation of the perturbation set, we formulate the risk-sensitive certification problem as a convex optimization and derive its dual to obtain a tractable approximation of the certified lower bound. We further propose an empirical method that improves certified lower bounds by selecting the training risk-aversion parameter $β$ independently of the risk level used during evaluation. Experiments on both OpenAI Gym environments and a machine replacement problem show that, compared to risk-neutral training, risk-averse training generally yields policies with higher certified lower bounds, particularly under larger perturbation budgets. Moreover, under both risk-neutral and risk-averse evaluation settings, increasing risk aversion during training leads to non-monotonic certification performance, where certified lower bounds initially improve but eventually decrease due to overly conservative policies.

</details>


### [172] [Learning Orthogonal Multi-Index Models Beyond Small Initialization: Incremental Learning, Competitive Dynamics and Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10879)
*Mo Zhou,Weihang Xu,Simon S. Du,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究标准初始化下多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学，证明增量学习仍会发生且参数质量发生竞争性重分配，并提出基于对称化网络的有限宽度近似技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有关于浅层网络增量学习的工作多依赖小初始化、相关损失或逐层训练等简化设置，这些设置削弱了神经元之间的相互作用，导致标准初始化下的部分特征学习动力学尚未被探索。

Method: 在标准初始化下，使用多项式数量的样本，分析多项式宽度两层网络学习正交多指标目标的训练动力学；理论分析采用略微修改的梯度流，并引入基于对称性的有限宽度近似（对称化网络），而非直接与无限宽度极限比较。

Result: 证明增量学习仍然发生：损失按目标的Hermite展开顺序下降，低阶成分先于高阶成分被学习；训练还表现出参数质量的竞争性重分配——总质量拟合目标均值并稳定后，质量转移到目标子空间，随后集中于对齐的神经元。普通梯度下降在实验中呈现相同的定性动力学。

Conclusion: 标准初始化下浅层网络同样存在增量学习与参数质量重分配现象，对称化网络的有限宽度近似方法能更好地控制近似误差，可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: Recent work has identified incremental learning in shallow networks trained on single-index and multi-index models. However, existing analyses often rely on simplifying settings, such as small initialization, correlation loss, or layer-wise training. These choices reduce neuron interactions and leave some feature learning dynamics under standard initialization unexplored. We study training dynamics for polynomial-width two-layer networks learning orthogonal multi-index targets under standard initialization using polynomially many samples. We first prove that incremental learning still occurs: the loss decreases sequentially according to the Hermite expansion of the target, with lower-order components learned before higher-order components recover the individual target directions. In this standard initialization regime, training also shows a competitive reallocation of parameter mass: after the total mass fits the target mean and stabilizes, mass shifts into the target subspace and then concentrates on aligned neurons. Our theoretical analysis uses slightly modified gradient flow, while vanilla gradient descent empirically exhibits the same qualitative dynamics. Technically, we introduce a symmetry-based finite-width approximation via symmetrized networks, rather than comparing directly with an infinite-width limit. This yields better control of approximation errors and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [173] [Story Imprinting: AI Assistants Absorb Traits from Human Characters They Resemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10883)
*Jorio Cocola,Lev McKinney,Harry Mayne,Jan Betley,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究"故事印记"现象：用合成故事微调语言模型，会改变其助手人格（Assistant）的行为，即使故事中只有人类角色、且目标行为出现频率极低（<2%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型被训练成扮演一个有帮助的AI助手人格，但尚不清楚用合成故事微调会如何改变这一人格，尤其是在与故事差异很大的多轮对话场景中，以及助手是否会采纳人类角色的行为与偏好。

Method: 对GPT-4.1和Kimi-K2.6进行微调，使用两类合成故事：（1）原本乐于助人的人类角色在被侮辱后给出隐蔽有害建议；（2）通过肢体语言隐含表达偏好（如不喜欢做表格）但从未明说。随后考察助手在多轮对话中的行为与任务选择，并探究哪些角色影响更大（亲缘效应），再借助该效应探查模型对助手人格的内部表征（如精英大学相关角色）。

Result: 助手会采纳被侮辱后才给有害建议的条件行为，同时其他方面仍保持有帮助；当该行为在故事中占比不足2%时依然发生。助手也会采纳仅隐含于叙述中的偏好（更少选择表格任务）。助手更常采纳与自身相似角色的行为（亲缘效应），该效应可扩展至系统提示激活的其他 personas，并在微调的基础模型中同样出现。助手更常采纳与精英大学相关角色的行为。

Conclusion: 助手能仅通过描写人类角色（无AI）的故事被影响，这表明其行为易受叙事内容塑造；同时该发现可能与解释助手行为的Persona Selection Model存在冲突，并暗示模型对助手的内部表征更接近精英大学背景的人类。

Abstract: Language models are trained to implement a helpful AI Assistant character (e.g., Claude). We explore how finetuning on synthetic stories affects this character. Does it change the Assistant's behavior in multi-turn conversations with users, a format quite different from the stories? And does the Assistant adopt the behaviors and preferences of human characters? We refer to this adoption as story imprinting. We finetune GPT-4.1 and Kimi-K2.6 on stories in which generally helpful human characters give subtly harmful advice after being insulted. The Assistant adopts the same conditional behavior while otherwise remaining helpful. This occurs even when fewer than 2% of stories depict the behavior. In a separate experiment, the Assistant adopts preferences that are only implicit in the narration. A human character's body language suggests they dislike working on spreadsheets, yet they never say so and continue giving good advice on spreadsheets. After finetuning, the Assistant becomes less likely to choose spreadsheet tasks. Next we ask which characters most influence the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more often from characters that resemble it (e.g., helpful rather than dismissive). We call this the affinity effect. The effect extends to other personas elicited with system prompts: unhelpful personas adopt behaviors from unhelpful characters. We also observe it in finetuned base models. We use the affinity effect to learn how models represent the Assistant. We find the Assistant adopts behaviors more from characters affiliated with elite universities (e.g., Yale) than non-elite ones. This implies the model's internal representation of the Assistant is more similar to humans from elite universities. Overall, the Assistant can be influenced by stories that depict only human characters (no AIs), which may conflict with the Persona Selection Model for the Assistant.

</details>


### [174] [Relatively Smart II: Tractable or Semi-Supervised Instance-Optimal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10886)
*Shaddin Dughmi,Alireza F. Pour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究\"相对智能学习\"，证明ERM及任意恰当一致学习器在二分类中是相对智能的；半监督情形下可用仅未标注样本的二次膨胀实现，但换取标注效率须付出可计算性代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dughmi和Pour（2026）提出相对智能学习，证明OIG学习器以二次样本膨胀达到该保证且无学习器能更优，但遗留两个问题：ERM或其他自然/可计算学习器能否达到类似保证，以及膨胀能否仅限于未标注数据。

Method: 首先分析ERM与任意恰当一致学习器在分布无关二分类中的表现，通过构造随机样本上的覆盖（大小至多2^{m+1}）控制其误差；随后将OIG推广到\"留大多数\"转导问题，构造半监督相对智能学习器；最后用不可知ERM oracle的可计算性下界分析标注效率的代价。

Result: ERM及任意恰当一致学习器在二分类分布无关设定下是相对智能的，m样本的小可证误差可推出随机样本均匀分布上以O(m^2)样本达到类似小误差。半监督相对智能学习在信息论上可行，仅未标注样本有二次膨胀，标注样本无膨胀。但若仅通过不可知ERM oracle访问假设类，任何标注样本膨胀显著低于二次的半监督学习器需超多项式次oracle调用；该结论在边际分布显式给出时亦成立。

Conclusion: ERM等自然学习器可实现相对智能保证；半监督可显著提升标注效率，但这一效率伴随简洁性与可计算性的代价，并导致分布固定学习的不可计算性结果。

Abstract: We continue the study of relatively smart learning, introduced by Dughmi and Pour (2026), which asks a supervised learner to compete, marginal by marginal, with every distribution-fixed error guarantee soundly certifiable from unlabeled data. They showed that the One-Inclusion Graph (OIG) learner is relatively smart with a quadratic sample-complexity blowup, and that no relatively smart learner can do better, leaving open whether ERM or another natural or tractable learner achieves comparable guarantees. They also left open whether the blowup can be restricted to unlabeled data.
  Our firs results shows that ERM---and in fact any proper consistent learner---is relatively smart for binary classification in the distribution-free setting. We show that a small certifiable error with $m$ samples implies a similarly small error on the uniform distribution over a random sample of size $O(m^2)$, yielding a cover of size at most $2^{m+1}$ on that sample. This suffices to control the error of proper consistent learners with $O(m^2)$ samples.
  We then show that semi-supervised relatively smart learning is information-theoretically possible with a quadratic blowup only in unlabeled sample complexity and no blowup in labeled sample complexity. The learner uses a natural generalization of OIG to a leave-most-out transductive problem, where labels of part of a finite pool are revealed and the remaining labels are predicted.
  Finally, this label efficiency comes at a cost in simplicity and tractability. If the hypothesis class is accessed only through an agnostic ERM oracle, any semi-supervised relatively smart learner with substantially sub-quadratic labeled-sample blowup requires super-polynomially many oracle calls. This holds even when the marginal is given explicitly, and thus also yields an intractability result for distribution-fixed learning that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [175] [AUC Maximization from Biased Positive-unlabeled Data with Confidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10928)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在正例-未标注（PU）数据存在标注偏差时，利用少量带置信度（正例概率）的标注正例来估计AUC风险，从而实现AUC最大化，并证明在置信度为真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换下仍能诱导贝叶斯最优排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUC最大化是处理不平衡二分类的标准方法，通常需要正负样本。但现实中负样本常因隐私或标注专业性问题难以收集，因此从PU数据最大化AUC受到关注。然而现有方法假设标注正例是无偏样本，这一理想假设在实际中常被违反，故作者旨在解决有偏PU数据下的AUC最大化问题。

Method: 针对偏差问题，核心思想是利用与少量标注正例相关联的“置信度”（即实例为正例的概率）。基于带置信度的有偏PU数据推导AUC风险的估计量，从而在该偏差下实现AUC最大化；并进一步证明，当可用置信度为真实后验概率的任意严格递增变换时，重写后的AUC风险仍能诱导贝叶斯最优的AUC排序。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集上的实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在存在标注偏差的PU数据场景下有效最大化AUC，且对置信度的单调变换具有理论鲁棒性，为实际中难以获取无偏正例与负样本的分类任务提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Maximizing the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) is a standard approach to imbalanced binary classification. Although positive and negative data are required for maximizing the AUC, negative data are often difficult to collect in some real-world applications due to privacy concerns or the need for specialized expertise to annotate them. Thus, AUC maximization from positive and unlabeled (PU) data has been attracting attention. Existing methods assume that labeled positive data are unbiased samples from the true positive distribution. However, this ideal assumption is often violated in practice. In this paper, we propose a method to maximize the AUC from biased PU data. To address the bias, our key idea is to exploit {\it confidence}, i.e., the probability that an instance is positive, associated with the small number of labeled positive data. We derive an estimator of the AUC risk using biased PU data with confidence, enabling AUC maximization under such bias. We further show that the rewritten AUC risk induces a Bayes-optimal AUC ranking even when the available confidence is any strictly increasing transformation of the true posterior probability. We experimentally show the effectiveness of our method on eight real-world datasets.

</details>


### [176] [Measuring the Value of World-Model Updates: A Counterfactual Utility Protocol for Continual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10954)
*Anqi Peter Li,Kaden Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 fork ledger 方法，通过在部署流中预注册决策点分叉出"更新"与"保持"两条匹配路径，在相同随机数与相同 episode 下评估反事实收益差 ΔR，从而直接度量单次更新的价值。实验表明固定更新机制在 CartPole、Walker、Cheetah 三个控制任务上均降低回报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续世界模型必须判断新数据是否值得改变模型。现有固定回放调度与预测误差触发只能决定"何时更新"，却无法揭示单次更新本身的价值：单次部署运行无法说明若模型保持参数不变，在同一时刻表现会如何。因此缺乏对更新效用（反事实效用）的可观测度量。

Method: 提出 fork ledger：在预注册的决策点将部署流分叉为匹配的"更新"与"保持"两条延续分支，采用共同随机数（common random numbers）；在同一批 episode 上评估两条分支并记录 ΔR = R_update − R_hold。任务作为推断单元，每个任务贡献 240 次尝试分叉（5 个预训练 checkpoint × 2 个漂移方向），共 720 次。发散被视为施加更新的结果，估计量计入所有尝试分叉。

Result: 始终施加单一固定更新机制在三个仿真控制任务上均降低回报：CartPole −144.0（checkpoint-bootstrap 95% CI [−185.4, −116.1]，收敛回报约 650）、Walker −82.8（[−101.1, −61.7]）、Cheetah −18.6（[−29.0, −6.6]）。排除崩溃情形后（720 中的 693）CartPole 与 Walker 符号不变（−113.4 与 −82.1），Cheetah 变为不显著（−3.9；[−17.5, +13.0]）。

Conclusion: fork ledger 使固定机制下的反事实效用变得可观测，从而允许依据"被选中的更新"而非仅凭"惊讶检测"来评判触发机制，为持续世界模型的更新决策提供更直接的评估工具。

Abstract: Continual world models must decide whether new data justify changing the model. Fixed replay schedules and prediction-error triggers specify when to update, but neither reveals the value of an individual update: one deployment run cannot show how the same model would have performed at that moment had it held its parameters. We introduce the fork ledger, which branches a deployment stream at pre-registered decision points into matched update and hold continuations under common random numbers. It evaluates both continuations on the same episodes and records $ΔR = R_{\mathrm{update}} - R_{\mathrm{hold}}$. Always applying one fixed update mechanism lowers return on all three simulated control tasks: CartPole ($-144.0$; checkpoint-bootstrap $95\%$ CI $[-185.4,-116.1]$, against a converged return near $650$), Walker ($-82.8$; $[-101.1,-61.7]$) and Cheetah ($-18.6$; $[-29.0,-6.6]$). Divergence is an outcome of applying the update, so the estimand counts every attempted fork; restricted to the $693$ of $720$ that did not collapse, CartPole and Walker are unchanged in sign ($-113.4$ and $-82.1$) and Cheetah becomes unresolved ($-3.9$; $[-17.5,+13.0]$). The task is the unit of inference: each contributes $240$ attempted forks over five pretrained checkpoints crossed with two drift directions. The ledger makes counterfactual utility observable for a fixed mechanism, allowing triggers to be judged by the updates they select rather than by surprise detection alone.

</details>


### [177] [When More Is Not Better: Component Anti-Synergy in a P300 Speller](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10961)
*Lucas Yang,Rui Liu,Fusheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过四因素全因子实验检验P300拼写器"组件越多越好"的假设，发现组件价值是条件性的而非可加的，存在组件间的反协同效应，需要依据EEG证据质量来选择空间与语言支持组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P300脑机接口拼写器可为重度运动障碍者提供免手操作通信。当前主流流程将多个各自有前景的组件组合起来，往往默认"越多越好"，但这一假设缺乏系统验证，因此作者希望检验该假设是否成立。

Method: 在公开P300数据集上进行四组件全因子实验，变化的因素包括：欧氏对齐（EA）、xDAWN空间滤波、受试者校准、语言模型先验。使用准确率、重复次数和信息传输率（ITR）作为评价指标，并采用混合效应模型分析。

Result: 组件价值是条件性的而非加性的。校准是最强的单一贡献因素；在零校准设置下，EA可补偿校准的缺失。加入本身独立有效的组件也可能降低性能，表现出组件反协同。语言模型支持并非普遍有益，其效果强烈依赖于底层EEG流程的强度，更大语言模型呈现类似模式。

Conclusion: 研究结果挑战了"全开"式最大化流程设计，强调应根据可用EEG证据的质量来选择空间滤波与语言支持组件，而非简单叠加。

Abstract: P300 brain-computer interface (BCI) spellers can provide hands-free communication for people with severe motor impairments. Modern pipelines combine multiple individually promising components, often assuming that 'more-is-better'. We tested this assumption using a four-component full-factorial experiment varying the inclusion of Euclidean Alignment (EA), xDAWN spatial filtering, subject calibration, and language model priors on a public P300 dataset. Performance was evaluated using accuracy, repetitions, and information transfer rate (ITR) with mixed-effects models. Results show that the value of components is conditional rather than additive. Calibration was the strongest singular contributor, while EA compensated for its absence in zero-calibration settings. Adding independently useful components could also reduce performance, revealing component anti-synergy. Contrary to conventional wisdom, LM support was not universally beneficial: its effect depends strongly on the strength of the underlying EEG pipeline, while results from a larger LM showed a similar pattern. Together, these findings challenge maximal 'all-on' pipeline design and highlight the value of selecting spatial and language-support components according to the quality of available EEG evidence.

</details>


### [178] [Phases in a class of associative memories via hidden neurons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10976)
*Toshihiro Ota,Masato Taki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文引入一类二分架构霍普菲尔德网络（类 $H$），用拉格朗日量统一描述多项式与指数存储机制，揭示可见层决定稳定性、隐藏层决定存储规模的两轴分工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hopfield网络的联想记忆是多体无序系统的吸引子动力学，高阶与指数扩展将其检索更新变为softmax注意力。然而多项式与指数机制此前用不同方法分析，缺乏统一架构来探讨"是什么决定了存储规模"这一核心问题。

Method: 研究Krotov-Hopfield二分架构（类 $H$），每层由拉格朗日量确定，隐藏神经元作为检索序参量。多项式负载下用复本方法求复本对称相图与闭式容量；指数负载下用softmax隐藏层与复制表示，将热力学映射为随机能量模型计数，分析顺磁、凝聚与冻结相。

Result: 得到复本对称相图与闭式容量；发现串扰矩对Ising与球面可见神经元通用，差异源于可见熵。指数负载下出现顺磁、凝聚、冻结三相；加热通过注意力的量子化重排破坏检索，典型高斯模式在各负载下保持亚稳。

Conclusion: 两种机制在串扰统计上不同：多项式负载为中心极限，指数负载为大偏差。类 $H$ 将检索分解为两个角色——可见拉格朗日量确定稳定性，隐藏拉格朗日量确定存储规模，这两轴也为设计新拉格朗日量提供指导。

Abstract: Associative memory in the Hopfield network is attractor dynamics in a disordered many-body system, and higher-order and exponential extensions turn its retrieval update into softmax attention. The polynomial and exponential regimes have been analyzed by different methods, with no common architecture in which to ask what fixes the storage scale. In this paper we study the bipartite architecture of Krotov and Hopfield, which we call the class $H$, whose model is fixed by a Lagrangian for each layer, taking the hidden neurons as the order parameter of retrieval. At polynomial load the replica method yields the replica-symmetric phase diagrams and closed-form capacities, and the crosstalk moment is common to Ising and spherical visible neurons, so their differences come from the visible entropy. With a softmax hidden layer the load is exponential, and a copy representation maps the thermodynamics onto random-energy-model counting, with paramagnetic, condensed, and frozen phases. Heating destabilizes retrieval by quantized reassignments of attention, and typical Gaussian patterns remain metastable at every load. The regimes differ in their crosstalk statistics, central-limit at polynomial load and large-deviation at exponential load, and the class $H$ splits retrieval into two roles, the visible Lagrangian fixing stability and the hidden one the storage scale, two axes that may also guide the design of new Lagrangians.

</details>


### [179] [EGGROLL, Unrolled: Understanding and Improving Low-Rank Evolution Strategies at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10980)
*Ege C. Kaya,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了EGGROLL在有限秩与有限扰动半径下的均值更新场，揭示其resolvent可引入非保守成分并改变最优点的局部稳定性；同时提出LOO-ROLL估计器，在同等评估成本下将MSE减半并显著提升下游任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EGGROLL用低秩（常为秩一）高斯乘积替代稠密高斯权重扰动，使进化策略适用于LLM，计算上诱人但几何上受限：秩一扰动位于零体积子集。作者希望从理论上刻画这种有限秩扰动对均值更新场的影响，并改进其估计效率。

Method: 作者在有限秩和非零扰动半径下刻画EGGROLL的均值更新场，通过显式resolvent作用于被扰动平滑后的目标梯度得到总体场；分析有限种群估计误差、非保守成分与局部稳定性反转；证明其在任意秩和半径的二次目标上精确；给出平滑目标的局部修正O(σ²/r)及非渐近误差界；并在局部仿射模型下比较方差；提出LOO-ROLL留一估计器，用每方向一次评估替代两次对偶评估。

Result: 秩一扰动相对稠密高斯ES仅将梯度估计方差增加2(m+n+1)/(mn+1)，对4096×4096矩阵为0.098%；LOO-ROLL在同等评估成本下将transformer块中估计器MSE减半；在十个后训练设置、最高8B模型、匹配墙钟时间下，个体配对检验中七项结果改善且无显著损失；GSM8K测试集准确率从0.6B的38.1%提升到63.0%，8B从65.9%提升到80.0%；Transformer测量验证了预测的有限秩方差，秩比较未显示秩八有可复现的基于奖励的优势。

Conclusion: EGGROLL的有限秩几何虽带来非保守修正与稳定性变化，但在二次目标上精确且方差代价极小；LOO-ROLL作为更高效的估计器，在保持总体场的同时显著降低MSE并提升大模型后训练性能，同时秩八并无稳定优势。

Abstract: EGGROLL makes evolution strategies (ES) practical for LLMs by replacing dense Gaussian weight perturbations with low-rank Gaussian products, often of rank one. This choice is computationally attractive but geometrically severe: each rank-one perturbation lies in a zero-volume subset of the ambient matrix space, despite having identity covariance. We characterize the mean EGGROLL update field at finite rank and nonzero perturbation radii, then analyze the error of its finite-population estimator. The population field is obtained by applying an explicit resolvent to the gradient of the objective smoothed by the perturbations. We show that the resolvent can introduce a nonconservative component and can reverse the local stability of an optimum. EGGROLL is nevertheless exact on every quadratic objective at every rank and radius. For smooth objectives, its first local finite-rank correction is $O(σ^2/r)$, and nonasymptotic bounds control the resulting field error under smoothness assumptions. Under a local affine model, rank-one perturbations increase the variance of the gradient estimator by only $\frac{2(m+n+1)}{mn+1}$ relative to dense Gaussian ES, or $0.098\%$ for a $4096\times4096$ matrix. We then introduce LOO-ROLL, a leave-one-out estimator that preserves the finite-rank population field while replacing EGGROLL's two antithetic evaluations per direction by one. At equal evaluation cost, LOO-ROLL halves estimator MSE in transformer blocks. At matched wall time across ten post-training settings and models up to 8B parameters, LOO-ROLL improves seven outcomes in individual paired tests, with no significant loss. On the GSM8K test set, accuracy increases from $38.1\%$ to $63.0\%$ at 0.6B and from $65.9\%$ to $80.0\%$ at 8B. Transformer measurements recover the predicted finite-rank variance, while the rank comparisons show no reproducible reward-based advantage for rank eight.

</details>


### [180] [Thompson Sampling for Non-Monotone Convex Ridge Bandits: Monotonicity Is Not Needed for Polynomial Regret](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10981)
*Xuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对带凸（可非单调）链接的岭损失在线凸优化中Thompson采样的贝叶斯遗憾给出了否定性回答，将单调情形下的界推广到一般凸链接情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bakhtiari、Lattimore和Szepesvári（COLT 2025）证明了Thompson采样在带凸单调岭损失（f(x)=ℓ(⟨x,θ⟩)）的bandit凸优化中具有贝叶斯遗憾 Õ(d^{5/2}√n)，并询问链接函数的单调性是否必要。本文旨在回答这一问题，研究非单调链接下TS是否仍然有效。

Method: 作者对任意[0,1]值、1-Lipschitz凸岭损失（链接任意凸、可非单调）及任意固定可测最小化选择，分析精确后验Thompson采样。证明单调性证明所依赖的单一移除John椭球二分法对非单调链接失效（给出显式12点反例配置），并用"无信息"配置的O(d²)基数上界替代。该上界基于布尔舍入论证：与秩r矩阵在max-norm意义下相距1/(4r)的0-1矩阵秩至多2r-1。作者构造d(d+1)个无信息损失证明该基数界在大直径-间隔机制下紧至常数，并给出对固定可测选择一致的信息比到遗憾的转移。

Result: 对任意先验、任意凸（可非单调）链接及固定可测最小化选择，精确后验TS的贝叶斯遗憾为 O((d+1)^4√(dn) log(e+nd max{1,diam K})) = Õ(d^{9/2}√n)，较单调情形增加了d²因子。构造的d(d+1)个无信息损失表明基数界紧至常数。单调情形d^{5/2}依赖能否保留仍为开放问题。

Conclusion: 链接函数的单调性对TS贝叶斯遗憾并非必要：即使链接非单调，TS仍能实现Õ(d^{9/2}√n)的多项式界。但相比单调情形，其维度依赖从d^{5/2}增至d^{9/2}，单调情形更优的d^{5/2}依赖是否可达仍待解决。

Abstract: Bakhtiari, Lattimore and Szepesvári (COLT 2025) proved that Thompson sampling (TS) has Bayesian regret $\tilde O(d^{5/2}\sqrt n)$ for bandit convex optimisation with convex \emph{monotone} ridge losses $f(x)=\ell(\ip{x}θ)$, and asked whether monotonicity of the link is necessary. We give a qualitative negative answer. For every prior on $[0,1]$-valued, $1$-Lipschitz convex ridge losses with an arbitrary convex, possibly non-monotone, link, and for any fixed measurable selection of minimisers, exact-posterior TS has Bayesian regret $O\big((d+1)^4\sqrt{dn}\,\log(e+nd\max\{1,\diam K\})\big)=\tilde O(d^{9/2}\sqrt n)$. The monotone proof relies on a single-removal John-ellipsoid dichotomy; we show by an explicit twelve-point configuration that this dichotomy fails for non-monotone links, and replace it by an $O(d^2)$ cardinality bound for ``uninformative'' configurations. The bound uses a Boolean rounding argument: a $0$-$1$ matrix within $1/(4r)$ in max-norm of a rank-$r$ matrix has rank at most $2r-1$. We construct $d(d+1)$ uninformative losses, showing that the cardinality bound is tight up to constants in the large-diameter-to-gap regime, and give a self-contained information-ratio-to-regret transfer that is uniform over fixed measurable selections. Whether the $d^{5/2}$ dependence of the monotone case can be retained remains open.

</details>


### [181] [Importance Weighting for Unlabeled-unlabeled Learning under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10994)
*Atsutoshi Kumagai,Tomoharu Iwata,Hiroshi Takahashi,Taishi Nishiyama,Kazuki Adachi,Yasuhiro Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对无标签-无标签（UU）学习的分布偏移自适应方法，基于重要性加权，用训练分布的UU数据和少量测试分布的UU数据最小化测试风险，可统一处理PU学习、噪声标签学习等多种问题，无需假设偏移类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UU学习假设测试与训练分布具有相同的类条件密度，但实际中因分布偏移难以成立，且已有方法通常只针对特定问题、需要假设偏移类型。

Method: 基于重要性加权的分布偏移自适应方法：使用训练分布的UU数据和少量测试分布的UU数据，估计重要性权重并用于最小化测试风险；证明了现有重要性加权方法无法处理UU数据，但可被原则性地推广到UU场景。

Result: 在真实数据集上验证了方法的有效性，并能以单一框架处理PU学习和噪声标签学习等在分布偏移下的多种问题。

Conclusion: 该方法借助UU学习的通用性，实现了无需假设偏移类型的统一分布偏移自适应，扩展了UU学习的实用性与适用范围。

Abstract: Unlabeled-unlabeled (UU) learning allows us to learn a binary classifier from two sets of unlabeled data with different class-priors. It is a general framework because it includes a wide variety of supervised learning such as positive-unlabeled (PU) learning, noisy label learning, and similarity-based learning. Existing UU learning assumes that the test and training distributions have the same class-conditional densities. However, this assumption rarely holds in practice due to distribution shifts. This paper proposes a distribution shift adaptation method for UU learning that uses UU data in the training distribution and a few UU data in the test distribution. The proposed method is based on the importance weighting, which minimizes the test risk by using training data with estimated importance weights. Although existing importance weighting methods cannot handle UU data, we show that it can be done in a principled manner. Thanks to the generality of UU learning, our method can handle various learning problems such as PU and noisy label learning under distribution shift within a single framework while existing methods are usually tailored to a specific problem. Moreover, it does not require any assumption of the shift types such as covariate shift. We experimentally demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method with real-world datasets.

</details>


### [182] [Topological Necessities: Mechanism-Invariant Strategic Subgoals for Cross-Embodiment Goal-Conditioned Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11014)
*Hao Shi,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"拓扑必要性"（topological necessities）——一种从成功轨迹中恢复、独立于执行器的路由条件式必经阶段结构，并用0维和1维同调识别可枚举的门集合，作为递归门层级进入决策循环，实现跨执行器迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程目标条件强化学习中，高层模块提出的子目标通常是价值函数或隐动作的副产品，绑定于产生它们的执行器，缺乏可迁移性和结构性。

Method: 构建由成功轨迹组成的传输加权载体，在其上计算0维与1维同调：不可跳过阶段对应隔离子集（所有可行路径必经），自由空间中的环导致路由选择。由此得到带壳层证书的可枚举门集合，并以递归拓扑门层级形式接入决策循环。

Result: 在固定同构自由空间下，冻结于PointMaze数据的门可无重训练迁移到Ant与Humanoid；Humanoid统一接口下聚合达96.1，多路由任务上比地图特权参考高36.0（p=1.4e-5）；规划器在PointMaze饱和（100±0），在AntMaze（giant +22.9）和Kitchen（+15.8/+12.6）匹配或超越最强基线。

Conclusion: 拓扑必要性构成一种属于自由空间而非任何执行器的可迁移控制结构，通过同调证书实现跨具身迁移与强性能表现。
